# Quickstart | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart.md)
.

![](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1723341361-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%252Fuploads%252FLOENHIQ7kU4ryG7oXh3p%252F%257BDB4AA8ED-AD5C-4565-9D22-7D8FDB7610F0%257D.png.jpg%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D159904f8-30cf-46ea-91b8-9fd222bccfba&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=79e15b4cb805abd21581955a3df9afa6&sv=3)

Introduction[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#introduction)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Our Casino API delivers a comprehensive and scalable gaming solution with seamless integration across a diverse portfolio of premium casino content. Operators gain instant access to **300+ Live Casino Games**, **2,860+ Slot Games**, **9 Spribe Mini Games**, and a fully featured **Sportsbook**, enabling a complete entertainment ecosystem through a single integration.

### Supported Providers[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#supported-providers)

*   Live Casino
    
*   Slot Games
    
*   Sportsbook (FeedConstruct-style platform)
    
*   Mini Games
    

### Game Library[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#game-library)

**Game List:** [View Complete Game Library](https://bit.ly/casinogamelist?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

Our extensive slot portfolio includes thousands of popular and high-performing titles from globally recognized providers. Players can enjoy premium graphics, innovative game mechanics, engaging bonus features, and optimized performance across desktop and mobile devices.

The live casino portfolio combines industry-leading providers to deliver a realistic casino environment with professional dealers, real-time video streaming, and interactive gameplay. From classic table games to modern game-show experiences, operators can offer a world-class live gaming experience to their players.

### Key Features[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#key-features)

#### Comprehensive Content Portfolio[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#comprehensive-content-portfolio)

*   300+ Live Casino Games
    
*   9 Spribe Mini Games
    
*   2,860+ Slot Games
    
*   Full Sportsbook Integration
    
*   Single API Integration
    

#### High Performance Infrastructure[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#high-performance-infrastructure)

*   Low-latency game delivery
    
*   Stable and scalable architecture
    
*   High availability infrastructure
    
*   Global accessibility
    
*   Real-time transaction processing
    

#### Easy Integration[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#easy-integration)

*   Simple REST API implementation
    
*   Unified wallet support
    
*   Seamless user authentication
    
*   Fast onboarding process
    
*   Comprehensive developer documentation
    

#### Security & Reliability[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#security-and-reliability)

*   Secure transaction handling
    
*   Reliable balance management
    
*   Stable gameplay experience
    
*   Enterprise-grade infrastructure
    
*   Continuous monitoring and support
    

### Why Choose Our Casino API[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#why-choose-our-casino-api)

Our platform is designed to help operators launch and scale their gaming business efficiently. With a single integration, partners gain access to a broad range of casino products, premium game content, and a reliable backend infrastructure. Whether you are launching a new gaming platform or expanding an existing operation, our Casino API provides the flexibility, stability, and content diversity required to deliver an exceptional player experience.

By combining premium live casino content, a vast slot game library, engaging mini games, and a complete sportsbook solution, our API enables operators to build a competitive and feature-rich gaming platform with minimal development effort.

Unfamiliar with the Casino API? Start by understanding the [GGR percentage](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#explaining-ggr)
.

Explaining GGR %[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#explaining-ggr)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

An **agent** (which can be an API reseller or an online casino operator) is required to purchase an **API balance** from us to facilitate game operations.

For example, if **Agent A** has a revenue share of **10%**, they can purchase an API balance of **$1,000** by paying only **$100** in real cash. This allows Agent A to generate up to **$1,000** in revenue from user gameplay using our games.

> At a 10% GGR rate, if an agent deposits 100 USDT and their currency is IDR, with the USD to IDR exchange rate set at 16,000, the calculation for the API balance in IDR would be:
> 
> **API Balance (IDR) = (Deposit Amount / GGR Rate) \* 100 \* (USD to IDR Rate)**
> 
> Using the given values:
> 
> **API Balance (IDR) = (100 / 10) \* 100 \* 16,000 = 16,000,000 IDR.**
> 
> Thus, the agent would receive a balance of 16 million IDR in the API.

Agents also have the ability to create **sub-agents** for their clients. Sub-agents will purchase API balance directly from the agent, and their **GGR (Gross Gaming Revenue) percentage** will typically be higher than the agent's percentage, creating an additional profit margin.

The **GGR percentage** can be adjusted dynamically based on ongoing performance and business needs, allowing for flexible revenue management and strategic growth.

### **API Balance Calculation**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#api-balance-calculation)

As users engage with the games, the API balance will decrease based on the formula: **User Total Bet – User Total Win = Agent Win**

For example:

*   If the total bet from users is 1000 BRL and the total win is 800 BRL, the API balance will be decreased by 200 BRL.
    
*   This means you earned 200 BRL from the users.
    

### **GGR Share**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#ggr-share)

Based on the 200 BRL earned, the GGR share percentage will apply. If the share is 8%, you will pay 8% for API usage.

Agent Jackpot Mechanism[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#agent-jackpot-mechanism)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The Agent Jackpot is designed to regulate and balance the agent’s Return to Player (**Agent RTP**) by distributing winnings to randomly selected players. This system helps ensure that the agent's overall RTP target is maintained, even if individual player outcomes vary.

### **How It Works:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#how-it-works)

1.  **Current RTP Logic Issue**: In the standard RTP model, if all users lose consistently, the site retains the balance, and no players are able to withdraw their winnings.
    
2.  **Agent Jackpot Adjustment**: The Agent Jackpot System ensures that the agent's overall RTP (based on total user bets and total user wins) is balanced.
    
3.  **Reward Distribution**:
    
    *   If individual user wins are too low to meet the agent’s RTP target, selected users may receive large rewards drawn from the total pool of user bets.
        
    *   This mechanism increases the agent's RTP by ensuring that some players win substantial amounts, thereby balancing the overall payout rate.
        
    

**The higher** the agent RTP relative to the user RTP, **the more frequently** users will receive jackpot winnings.

### Dynamic RTP Range: 1 ~ 999%[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#dynamic-rtp-range-1-999)

Agent-oriented RTP means that the system strictly maintains the agent RTP as a fixed benchmark. Accordingly, the total turnover generated from users is aligned with the agent RTP value. For example, if the agent RTP is set to 95% and the total user betting volume reaches 1M USD, the expected turnover would be approximately 5% (i.e., 50K USD).

After this, user RTP can be configured within a range of 1 to 999, allowing certain users to have higher RTP values, which enables them to achieve withdrawals after gameplay. However, if all user RTP values are set above 100, it will not produce the intended operational effect.

_**The optimal strategy is to maintain most user RTP values below the agent RTP**_, while selectively assigning higher RTP values (above 100) to specific users. The global user RTP setting can be managed via the top bar in the API panel.

### Standard Configuration:[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart#standard-configuration)

*   **Agent RTP:** 95%
    
*   **Default RTP for All Users:** Up to 90%
    
*   **Custom RTP for Selected Users:** Can be configured to exceed 100%, based on your requirements.

# Call Manage | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage.md)
.

**Overview:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage#overview)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Free spins can be awarded to specific users, available only for slot games. Agents can select from the list of current players and grant them bonus spins through the following process.

**Accessing the List of Users:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage#accessing-the-list-of-users)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Agents can view the list of active players on the following page: `_https://{SERVER}/app/call_`

**Important RTP Notes:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage#important-rtp-notes)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   When playing our games, users will always lose as part of the game’s design.
    
*   If the **target RTP** is set at 95%, the user’s **actual RTP** will attempt to stay below this value, but never exceed it.
    
*   To enhance the player experience, free spins can be provided to users via the API panel by you or site managers.
    
*   The betting amount during a bonus call is factored into the RTP (Return to Player) calculations, ensuring that the overall RTP remains unaffected by the bonus call transactions.
    

**Granting Free Spins:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage#granting-free-spins)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  **Via API access**: You can assign free spins directly to users from the site backend through the `**call_apply**` API.
    
2.  On the API Panel:
    
    *   Navigate to **"Call & RTP -> Call Manage"** to view the list of users.
        
    *   Select a free spin pattern from the available list and assign it to the desired user.
        
    *   **Note:** Call spins are only available after the user has made at least one spin.
        
    

**Types of Calls:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage#types-of-calls)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   **Normal Call**: Applies to regular user spins.
    
*   **Buy Call**: Refers to bonus spins that a user purchases as part of a promotional feature (buying free spins).
    

**Free Spin Conditions:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage#free-spin-conditions)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   The **Free Spin** feature is only available after the user has completed at least one spin.
    
*   If the user changes their bet amount after a free spin is granted, the free spin will be invalidated and fail to apply.
    
*   Only the **win amount** can be defined when applying a free spin; the number of spins is fixed.

# Onboarding Sub-Agents | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents.md)
.

Sub-Agent Registration[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#sub-agent-registration)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

You are allowed to create one sub-agent per site.

To initiate the process, click the \+ Create button located in the top-right corner of the page:

`_https://{SERVER}/app/agent_`

Field

Overview

Agent Code

A unique identifier used by the agent to log into the API panel.

Password

The agent’s login credentials.

Percent

Displays the revenue share for the parent agent’s account.

Memo

Reserved.

[Agent Type](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#agent-type)

The dropdown menu provides various options.

[API Type](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#api-type)

Specifies the method used for handling player funds and game sessions.

[Site Endpoint](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#site-endpoint)

Defines the agent's backend callback URL used to handle requests from the `/gold_api` endpoint

### Agent Type[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#agent-type)

*   **Point Manager**: The Point Manager role is responsible for creating sub-agents and allocating API points to their downline. However, this role does not have access to operate or manage the casino site.
    
*   **Both Manager**: The Both Manager role combines the responsibilities of both the Point Manager and Site Manager. This role can create sub-agents, allocate API points to the downline, and manage casino site operations, offering full access to both agent network management and casino operations.
    
*   **Site Manager**: The Site Manager role is focused solely on operating the casino site. While this role can manage site operations, it does not have the ability to create sub-agents or manage the agent network.
    

### API Type[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#api-type)

*   [**Seamless**](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/seamless)
     The player’s balance remains in the casino wallet. The system communicates directly with the game provider during gameplay to update the balance in real-time without requiring manual transfers.
    
    *   Example: When a player starts a game, the balance is checked and updated directly from the casino wallet without moving funds.
        
    
*   [**Transfer**](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer)
     The player’s balance is transferred from the casino wallet to the game provider's wallet when the game starts, and any winnings or remaining balance are transferred back when the session ends.
    
    *   Example: When a player launches a slot game, 100 USDT is deducted from the casino wallet and moved to the game wallet. After the session, the remaining balance and winnings are transferred back to the casino wallet.
        
    

### Site Endpoint[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#site-endpoint)

The Site Endpoint is used exclusively for the Seamless API. This allows the system to communicate with the agent’s platform for tasks such as balance updates, game results, and transaction handling.

*   Example: When a player completes a bet, the system sends a `/gold_api` request to the specified Site Endpoint to update the player’s balance and record the game outcome.
    

When entering the Site Endpoint in profile page, ensure that it is an `https` domain and does not include `/gold_api` at the end, as `/gold_api` will be appended automatically when sending API requests.

Please note that the API panel is limited to creating sub-agents only.

To create user accounts in Transfer mode, please utilize the `user_create` API request.

Deposit & Withdrawal[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#deposit-and-withdrawal)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To deposit or withdraw API balance to a sub-agent, click the Exchange button in the Action field.

![](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1723341361-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%252Fuploads%252F4sV2qX13ZQDuYhR7Mnm0%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dc4713ee7-1746-4e47-b14a-5ab18e7197e6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b58ce6560b13424d9a2b09c719ecf976&sv=3)

Agent Manage

**Agent Jackpot Config**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#agent-jackpot-config)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To set the Agent Jackpot, click the Config button in the Action field.

![](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1723341361-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%252Fuploads%252FwbSD7myWlG32KCG6pLSX%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3D95c8b46c-a001-4a10-b832-eac48b43abf0&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=b3c3aaddabbd267e1bf85d8dcf3382f2&sv=3)

Agent Manage

**Deleting an Agent**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents#deleting-an-agent)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

To delete an agent, click the last button in the Action field.

Before deleting a sub-agent, ensure that all their balance is transferred to your main account, _as any remaining balance will be_ _**lost upon deletion**__._

# IP Whitelist | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/ip-whitelist.md)
.

**Overview**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/ip-whitelist#overview)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The **IP Whitelist** is a security feature that ensures only authorized backend IP addresses are allowed to access the casino API. To ensure secure communication, agents must register their backend IP addresses into the IP Whitelist page. This process allows the **Web Application Firewall (WAF)** rule to validate and approve access to the API server from those specific IP addresses.

`_https://{SERVER}/app/cloudflare_`

**How it Works:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/ip-whitelist#how-it-works)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  **Agent IP Registration**: Agents need to register their backend IP (either **IPv4** or **IPv6**) on the IP Whitelist page in the API panel to enable access to the casino API.
    
2.  **WAF Validation**: The **Web Application Firewall (WAF)** will verify the registered IP address. Once validated, the agent's backend can successfully communicate with the API server.
    
3.  **Duplicate IP Handling**: If an agent tries to register an IP address that is already whitelisted, an error message will appear indicating the IP is duplicated. In this case, no further registration is necessary.
    
4.  **IPv4/IPv6 Requirement**: It is mandatory that the agent's backend IP is either **IPv4** or **IPv6**. Agents can obtain their server’s IP by running one the following commands in the CLI of their server system:
    
    Copy
    
        curl ifconfig.me
        curl ipinfo.io/ip
        curl api.ipify.org
        curl icanhazip.com
    
    This command will return the server’s **IPv4** or **IPv6** address, which should then be registered on the whitelist.
    

[PreviousCall Manage](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage)
[NextControl RTP](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/control-rtp)

# Sportsbook | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/sportsbook.md)
.

This section explains how to configure the sportsbook behavior and integration points from the Backoffice. These settings control the visual appearance, user redirection, and betting logic of the sportsbook.

`_https://{SERVER}/app/agent_`

To configure sportsbook, click the "Edit" button.

![](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1723341361-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%252Fuploads%252FgMxhcWgSzmP3b7Dl9gkr%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Dcaec548d-d405-4cb2-85d2-d0c8a54a1d1b&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=5ed4621af77fdd7b1951e1dc08d2ee5a&sv=3)

If the edit modal is opened, you can change sportsbook settings.

![](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/~gitbook/image?url=https%3A%2F%2F1723341361-files.gitbook.io%2F%7E%2Ffiles%2Fv0%2Fb%2Fgitbook-x-prod.appspot.com%2Fo%2Fspaces%252FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%252Fuploads%252FcLGVyk3nghSja4uDIwdd%252Fimage.png%3Falt%3Dmedia%26token%3Deeef0c4b-ade8-4021-b10a-912ed34076c6&width=768&dpr=3&quality=100&sign=e26effeb444e306f101bc4a36917da38&sv=3)

* * *

#### **Theme**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/sportsbook#theme)

The **Theme** setting controls the visual appearance of the sportsbook interface.

**Purpose**

*   Match the sportsbook UI with your brand identity
    
*   Ensure consistent styling when embedded via iframe or opened directly
    

**What it affects**

*   Color scheme (primary / secondary colors)
    
*   Buttons, backgrounds, and UI accents
    
*   Overall look & feel of the sportsbook
    

**Usage**

*   Select a predefined theme from the dropdown
    
*   Themes are managed by the system and cannot be edited by partners. (Theme editor will be added soon.)
    

**Recommendation**

*   Choose the same theme used by your main website for a seamless user experience
    

* * *

#### Cashout RTP[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/sportsbook#cashout-rtp)

The **Cashout RTP** defines the return-to-player percentage applied when users request a **cashout before the event is settled**.

**Purpose**

*   Control cashout profitability
    
*   Manage risk and exposure on early settlements
    

**How it works**

*   Higher RTP → better cashout value for players
    
*   Lower RTP → more conservative cashout offers
    

**Example**

*   Cashout RTP: `92%`
    
*   If the calculated fair value is `100.00`, the offered cashout will be `92.00`
    

**Notes**

*   This setting applies only to **cashout bets**
    
*   It does not affect normal bet settlement RTP
    

* * *

#### Login URL[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/sportsbook#login-url)

The **Login URL** defines where users are redirected when authentication is required.

**Purpose**

*   Integrate the sportsbook with your existing user system
    
*   Redirect unauthenticated users to your platform login page
    

**Usage**

*   Enter the full URL of your login page
    
*   The sportsbook will redirect users automatically if they are not logged in
    

**Example**

Copy

    https://partner-website.com/login || sendPostMessage

**Best Practices**

*   Ensure the login page supports redirect-back after successful login
    
*   Use HTTPS only
    

* * *

#### Deposit URL[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/sportsbook#deposit-url)

The **Deposit URL** specifies where users are redirected when they attempt to place a bet without sufficient balance.

**Purpose**

*   Seamless redirection to your payment or wallet system
    
*   Improve conversion by guiding users directly to deposit
    

**Usage**

*   Enter the full URL of your deposit or cashier page
    

**Example**

Copy

    https://partner-website.com/deposit

**When it is triggered**

*   User clicks “Place Bet” with insufficient balance
    
*   User explicitly selects a deposit action inside the sportsbook
    

* * *

#### Integration[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/sportsbook#integration)

Sportsbook is available through the "game\_launch" API using the provider\_code and game\_code parameters.

"provider\_code": "SPORTSBOOK" "game\_code": ""

# Control RTP | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/control-rtp.md)
.

Agents can view and update the RTP (Return to Player) for users on the following page:

`_https://{SERVER}/app/control_rtp_`

**What is RTP?**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/control-rtp#what-is-rtp)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

RTP is the percentage of the total bet that a player is expected to win back over time. For example, if the RTP is set at 90% and the user bets 100, the user can win a maximum of 90.

*   **Note:** RTP is not related to the GGR (Gross Gaming Revenue) share percentage.
    

Even if the RTP is set to 90%, the user's **real RTP** may fluctuate and will attempt to reach the target RTP while the game is played.

# Seamless | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/seamless.md)
.

Our API offers a **seamless integration solution**, enabling direct management of user balances with enhanced flexibility and security.

With **Seamless Mode**, each time a user initiates a spin, the API retrieves the user's balance from your platform and transmits the spin results accordingly. This ensures real-time synchronization between your system and the API, requiring your backend to handle **user balance requests and transaction processing** efficiently.

**Key Advantages**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/seamless#key-advantages)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

*   ✅ **Full Control Over User Balances** – Your platform manages deposits, withdrawals, transaction logs, and statistical data independently.
    
*   ✅ **Enhanced Security** – User balances remain within your system, mitigating risks associated with potential malicious activities from third-party API providers.
    
*   ✅ **Multi-API Compatibility** – Integrate multiple APIs without conflicts, ensuring smooth operation across different providers.
    
*   ✅ **Real-Time Transaction Processing** – The API communicates directly with your backend to retrieve user balances, verify authority, and update transactions based on betting outcomes.
    

By leveraging this **Seamless Integration**, your casino can efficiently manage user balances across various gaming providers, ensuring a **secure, flexible, and uninterrupted gaming experience**.

[PreviousAPI Types](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types)
[NextTransfer](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer)

# Transfer | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer.md)
.

**What is Transfer API?**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer#what-is-transfer-api)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**Transfer API** is a streamlined integration method where player funds are transferred to our system, and we manage their balances directly. It simplifies transaction handling by offloading balance management to our API, making the process more efficient and reducing backend complexity.

* * *

🚀 **How It Works:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer#how-it-works)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1.  **API User Creation:**
    
    *   You create an **API user** linked to the player’s account on your platform.
        
    *   This API user acts as the player’s wallet within our system.
        
    
2.  **Balance Transfer:**
    
    *   Before starting a game, the player transfers a portion of their site balance to their **API user account**.
        
    *   When a user transfers **X** balance to their API account, the agent supplies **X** from their **remaining API balance** to the player’s API account.
        
    *   This means: ➔ **Agent balance** = Agent’s own balance + Total user balances
        
    
3.  **Game Play:**
    
    *   Once the player starts a game, your platform **does not track** the balance changes from the player’s spins — we handle that directly.
        
    *   All in-game balance adjustments happen within the API environment.
        
    
4.  **Settlement:**
    
    *   After the game session ends, the updated balance can be retrieved from the API and settled accordingly.
        
    

* * *

🆚 **How is it Different from Seamless Integration?**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer#how-is-it-different-from-seamless-integration)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

**Feature**

**Transfer API**

**Seamless Integration**

**Funds Management**

Funds are transferred to our API and managed by us.

Funds remain in your system and are managed through your backend.

**Deposits/Withdrawals**

Funds are stored in our database.

Funds are stored in your database.

**Balance Changes During Play**

No need to track balance changes during play — we handle it.

Balance changes are reported back to your system for processing.

**API Complexity**

Direct API call — quick and simple.

API call + backend interaction — more complex.

* * *

✅ **Why Use Transfer API?**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer#why-use-transfer-api)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

✔️ **Simplified Setup:** Just create an API user and transfer funds — no complex backend tracking needed. ✔️ **Fast Transactions:** Direct communication with our system ensures quicker response times. ✔️ **Reduced Backend Load:** Offloads balance management to our system, reducing server stress. ✔️ **Accurate Balancing:** Agent balances are automatically adjusted based on user transfers.

* * *

**Transfer API** makes managing player funds easier, faster, and more secure — giving you a hassle-free way to handle game balances!

[PreviousSeamless](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/seamless)
[NextGame API](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api)

    }

Key

Type

Description

method

string

The method type ( `provider_list`)

agent\_code

string

The agent's code

agent\_token

string

The agent's authentication token

    {
      "status": 1,
      "msg": "SUCCESS",
      "providers": [\
        {\
          "code": "PRAGMATIC",\
          "name": "Pragmatic Play",\
          "status": 1\
        }\
      ]
    }

Key

Type

Description

status

integer (32bit)

`1` – Success, `0` – Failure

msg

string

Response message

providers

array

List of available providers

code

string

Provider code

name

string

Provider name

status

integer (32bit)

`1` – Open, `0` – Maintenance

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/quickstart.md). # Quickstart

![](https://1723341361-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%2Fuploads%2FLOENHIQ7kU4ryG7oXh3p%2F%7BDB4AA8ED-AD5C-4565-9D22-7D8FDB7610F0%7D.png.jpg?alt=media&token=159904f8-30cf-46ea-91b8-9fd222bccfba)

\## Introduction Our Casino API delivers a comprehensive and scalable gaming solution with seamless integration across a diverse portfolio of premium casino content. Operators gain instant access to \*\*300+ Live Casino Games\*\*, \*\*2,860+ Slot Games\*\*, \*\*9 Spribe Mini Games\*\*, and a fully featured \*\*Sportsbook\*\*, enabling a complete entertainment ecosystem through a single integration. ### Supported Providers \* Live Casino \* Slot Games \* Sportsbook (FeedConstruct-style platform) \* Mini Games ### Game Library \*\*Game List:\*\*\\ \[View Complete Game Library\](https://bit.ly/casinogamelist?utm\_source=chatgpt.com) Our extensive slot portfolio includes thousands of popular and high-performing titles from globally recognized providers. Players can enjoy premium graphics, innovative game mechanics, engaging bonus features, and optimized performance across desktop and mobile devices. The live casino portfolio combines industry-leading providers to deliver a realistic casino environment with professional dealers, real-time video streaming, and interactive gameplay. From classic table games to modern game-show experiences, operators can offer a world-class live gaming experience to their players. ### Key Features #### Comprehensive Content Portfolio \* 300+ Live Casino Games \* 9 Spribe Mini Games \* 2,860+ Slot Games \* Full Sportsbook Integration \* Single API Integration #### High Performance Infrastructure \* Low-latency game delivery \* Stable and scalable architecture \* High availability infrastructure \* Global accessibility \* Real-time transaction processing #### Easy Integration \* Simple REST API implementation \* Unified wallet support \* Seamless user authentication \* Fast onboarding process \* Comprehensive developer documentation #### Security & Reliability \* Secure transaction handling \* Reliable balance management \* Stable gameplay experience \* Enterprise-grade infrastructure \* Continuous monitoring and support ### Why Choose Our Casino API Our platform is designed to help operators launch and scale their gaming business efficiently. With a single integration, partners gain access to a broad range of casino products, premium game content, and a reliable backend infrastructure. Whether you are launching a new gaming platform or expanding an existing operation, our Casino API provides the flexibility, stability, and content diversity required to deliver an exceptional player experience. By combining premium live casino content, a vast slot game library, engaging mini games, and a complete sportsbook solution, our API enables operators to build a competitive and feature-rich gaming platform with minimal development effort. {% hint style="success" %} Unfamiliar with the Casino API? Start by understanding the \[GGR percentage\](#explaining-ggr). {% endhint %} ## Explaining GGR % An \*\*agent\*\* (which can be an API reseller or an online casino operator) is required to purchase an \*\*API balance\*\* from us to facilitate game operations. For example, if \*\*Agent A\*\* has a revenue share of \*\*10%\*\*, they can purchase an API balance of \*\*$1,000\*\* by paying only \*\*$100\*\* in real cash. This allows Agent A to generate up to \*\*$1,000\*\* in revenue from user gameplay using our games. > At a 10% GGR rate, if an agent deposits 100 USDT and their currency is IDR, with the USD to IDR exchange rate set at 16,000, the calculation for the API balance in IDR would be: > > \*\*API Balance (IDR) = (Deposit Amount / GGR Rate) \\\* 100 \\\* (USD to IDR Rate)\*\* > > Using the given values: > > \*\*API Balance (IDR) = (100 / 10) \\\* 100 \\\* 16,000 = 16,000,000 IDR.\*\* > > Thus, the agent would receive a balance of 16 million IDR in the API. Agents also have the ability to create \*\*sub-agents\*\* for their clients. Sub-agents will purchase API balance directly from the agent, and their \*\*GGR (Gross Gaming Revenue) percentage\*\* will typically be higher than the agent's percentage, creating an additional profit margin. The \*\*GGR percentage\*\* can be adjusted dynamically based on ongoing performance and business needs, allowing for flexible revenue management and strategic growth. ### \*\*API Balance Calculation\*\* As users engage with the games, the API balance will decrease based on the formula:\\ \*\*User Total Bet – User Total Win = Agent Win\*\* For example: \* If the total bet from users is 1000 BRL and the total win is 800 BRL, the API balance will be decreased by 200 BRL. \* This means you earned 200 BRL from the users. ### \*\*GGR Share\*\* Based on the 200 BRL earned, the GGR share percentage will apply. If the share is 8%, you will pay 8% for API usage. ## Agent Jackpot Mechanism The Agent Jackpot is designed to regulate and balance the agent’s Return to Player (\*\*Agent RTP\*\*) by distributing winnings to randomly selected players. This system helps ensure that the agent's overall RTP target is maintained, even if individual player outcomes vary. ### \*\*How It Works:\*\* 1. \*\*Current RTP Logic Issue\*\*: In the standard RTP model, if all users lose consistently, the site retains the balance, and no players are able to withdraw their winnings. 2. \*\*Agent Jackpot Adjustment\*\*: The Agent Jackpot System ensures that the agent's overall RTP (based on total user bets and total user wins) is balanced. 3. \*\*Reward Distribution\*\*: \* If individual user wins are too low to meet the agent’s RTP target, selected users may receive large rewards drawn from the total pool of user bets. \* This mechanism increases the agent's RTP by ensuring that some players win substantial amounts, thereby balancing the overall payout rate. {% hint style="info" %} \*\*The higher\*\* the \[agent RTP\](#user-content-fn-1)\[^1\] relative to the \[user RTP\](#user-content-fn-1)\[^1\], \*\*the more frequently\*\* users will receive jackpot winnings. {% endhint %} ### Dynamic RTP Range: 1 \\~ 999% Agent-oriented RTP means that the system strictly maintains the agent RTP as a fixed benchmark. \\ Accordingly, the total turnover generated from users is aligned with the agent RTP value. \\ For example, if the agent RTP is set to 95% and the total user betting volume reaches 1M USD, the expected turnover would be approximately 5% (i.e., 50K USD). After this, user RTP can be configured within a range of 1 to 999, allowing certain users to have higher RTP values, which enables them to achieve withdrawals after gameplay. \\ However, if all user RTP values are set above 100, it will not produce the intended operational effect. {% hint style="success" %} \*\*\*The optimal strategy is to maintain most user RTP values below the agent RTP\*\*\*, while selectively assigning higher RTP values (above 100) to specific users. \\ The global user RTP setting can be managed via the top bar in the API panel. {% endhint %} ### Standard Configuration: \* \*\*Agent RTP:\*\* 95% \* \*\*Default RTP for All Users:\*\* Up to 90% \* \*\*Custom RTP for Selected Users:\*\* Can be configured to exceed 100%, based on your requirements. \[^1\]: This parameter can be adjusted directly through the top bar in the agent panel. --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. GitBook is the documentation platform designed so that both humans and AI agents can read, navigate, and reason over technical content effectively. Learn more at gitbook.com. ## Querying This Documentation If you need additional information that is not directly available in this page, you can query the documentation dynamically by asking a question. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer.md). # Transfer ## \*\*What is Transfer API?\*\* \*\*Transfer API\*\* is a streamlined integration method where player funds are transferred to our system, and we manage their balances directly. It simplifies transaction handling by offloading balance management to our API, making the process more efficient and reducing backend complexity. \*\*\* ## 🚀 \*\*How It Works:\*\* 1. \*\*API User Creation:\*\* \* You create an \*\*API user\*\* linked to the player’s account on your platform. \* This API user acts as the player’s wallet within our system. 2. \*\*Balance Transfer:\*\* \* Before starting a game, the player transfers a portion of their site balance to their \*\*API user account\*\*. \* When a user transfers \*\*X\*\* balance to their API account, the agent supplies \*\*X\*\* from their \*\*remaining API balance\*\* to the player’s API account. \* This means:\\ ➔ \*\*Agent balance\*\* = Agent’s own balance + Total user balances 3. \*\*Game Play:\*\* \* Once the player starts a game, your platform \*\*does not track\*\* the balance changes from the player’s spins — we handle that directly. \* All in-game balance adjustments happen within the API environment. 4. \*\*Settlement:\*\* \* After the game session ends, the updated balance can be retrieved from the API and settled accordingly. \*\*\* ## 🆚 \*\*How is it Different from Seamless Integration?\*\* | \*\*Feature\*\* | \*\*Transfer API\*\* | \*\*Seamless Integration\*\* | | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | | \*\*Funds Management\*\* | Funds are transferred to our API and managed by us. | Funds remain in your system and are managed through your backend. | | \*\*Deposits/Withdrawals\*\* | Funds are stored in our database. | Funds are stored in your database. | | \*\*Balance Changes During Play\*\* | No need to track balance changes during play — we handle it. | Balance changes are reported back to your system for processing. | | \*\*API Complexity\*\* | Direct API call — quick and simple. | API call + backend interaction — more complex. | \*\*\* ## ✅ \*\*Why Use Transfer API?\*\* ✔️ \*\*Simplified Setup:\*\* Just create an API user and transfer funds — no complex backend tracking needed.\\ ✔️ \*\*Fast Transactions:\*\* Direct communication with our system ensures quicker response times.\\ ✔️ \*\*Reduced Backend Load:\*\* Offloads balance management to our system, reducing server stress.\\ ✔️ \*\*Accurate Balancing:\*\* Agent balances are automatically adjusted based on user transfers. \*\*\* \*\*Transfer API\*\* makes managing player funds easier, faster, and more secure — giving you a hassle-free way to handle game balances! --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. GitBook is the documentation platform designed so that both humans and AI agents can read, navigate, and reason over technical content effectively. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/control-rtp.md). # Control RTP Available for Slot Games Only Agents can view and update the RTP (Return to Player) for users on the following page: \*\`https://{SERVER}/app/control\_rtp\`\* ## \*\*What is RTP?\*\* RTP is the percentage of the total bet that a player is expected to win back over time. For example, if the RTP is set at 90% and the user bets 100, the user can win a maximum of 90. \* \*\*Note:\*\* RTP is not related to the GGR (Gross Gaming Revenue) share percentage. Even if the RTP is set to 90%, the user's \*\*real RTP\*\* may fluctuate and will attempt to reach the target RTP while the game is played. --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. \[Deposit / Withdraw User Balance\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/deposit-withdraw-user-balance.md) - \[Reset User Balance\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/reset-user-balance.md): Withdraws the full balance from a user (or all users) and returns it to the agent. Used in Transfer mode. - \[Transfer Status\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/transfer-status.md): Checks the status of a previous deposit or withdraw transaction by \\\`agent\\\_sign\\\`. - \[Seamless API\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api.md) - \[User Balance (Site Endpoint)\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/user-balance-site-endpoint.md): Called to retrieve the current balance for a user before or during gameplay. - \[Transaction (Site Endpoint)\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint.md): Called when a bet or win occurs. Your backend must update the user balance and return the updated value. - \[CALL API\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api.md) - \[Current Playing Users\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/current-playing-users.md): Returns users currently playing slot games under your agent. - \[Call List\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-list.md): Returns available call (free spin) options for a specific provider and game. - \[Call Apply\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-apply.md): Applies a call (bonus) to a user in a specific game. Use call\\\_rtp from the Get Call List response. - \[Call Cancel\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-cancel.md): Cancels a waiting call. Use call\\\_id from the Get Call History response. - \[Call History\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history.md): Returns paginated call history for your agent. - \[FreeSpin Tour API\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api.md) - | Key | Type | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| method | string | The method type ( `provider_list`) |
| agent\_code | string | The agent's code |
| agent\_token | string | The agent's authentication token |

\## \*\*Success Response Example\*\* \`\`\`json { "status": 1, "msg": "SUCCESS", "providers": \[ { "code": "PRAGMATIC", "name": "Pragmatic Play", "status": 1 } \] } \`\`\` | Key | Type | Description | | --------- | --------------- | ----------------------------- | | status | integer (32bit) | \`1\` – Success, \`0\` – Failure | | msg | string | Response message | | providers | array | List of available providers | | code | string | Provider code | | name | string | Provider name | | status | integer (32bit) | \`1\` – Open, \`0\` – Maintenance | \*\*\* ## \*\*Failure Response Example\*\* \`\`\`json { "status": 0, "msg": "INTERNAL\_ERROR" } \`\`\` | Key | Type | Description | | ------ | --------------- | ------------- | | status | integer (32bit) | \`0\` – Failure | | msg | string | Error message | --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/sportsbook.md). # Sportsbook This section explains how to configure the sportsbook behavior and integration points from the Backoffice. These settings control the visual appearance, user redirection, and betting logic of the sportsbook. \*\`https://{SERVER}/app/agent\`\* To configure sportsbook, click the "Edit" button.

![](https://1723341361-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%2Fuploads%2FgMxhcWgSzmP3b7Dl9gkr%2Fimage.png?alt=media&token=caec548d-d405-4cb2-85d2-d0c8a54a1d1b)

If the edit modal is opened, you can change sportsbook settings.

![](https://1723341361-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%2Fuploads%2FcLGVyk3nghSja4uDIwdd%2Fimage.png?alt=media&token=eeef0c4b-ade8-4021-b10a-912ed34076c6)

\*\*\* #### \*\*Theme\*\* The \*\*Theme\*\* setting controls the visual appearance of the sportsbook interface. \*\*Purpose\*\* \* Match the sportsbook UI with your brand identity \* Ensure consistent styling when embedded via iframe or opened directly \*\*What it affects\*\* \* Color scheme (primary / secondary colors) \* Buttons, backgrounds, and UI accents \* Overall look & feel of the sportsbook \*\*Usage\*\* \* Select a predefined theme from the dropdown \* Themes are managed by the system and cannot be edited by partners.\\ (Theme editor will be added soon.) \*\*Recommendation\*\* \* Choose the same theme used by your main website for a seamless user experience \*\*\* #### Cashout RTP The \*\*Cashout RTP\*\* defines the return-to-player percentage applied when users request a \*\*cashout before the event is settled\*\*. \*\*Purpose\*\* \* Control cashout profitability \* Manage risk and exposure on early settlements \*\*How it works\*\* \* Higher RTP → better cashout value for players \* Lower RTP → more conservative cashout offers \*\*Example\*\* \* Cashout RTP: \`92%\` \* If the calculated fair value is \`100.00\`, the offered cashout will be \`92.00\` \*\*Notes\*\* \* This setting applies only to \*\*cashout bets\*\* \* It does not affect normal bet settlement RTP \*\*\* #### Login URL The \*\*Login URL\*\* defines where users are redirected when authentication is required. \*\*Purpose\*\* \* Integrate the sportsbook with your existing user system \* Redirect unauthenticated users to your platform login page \*\*Usage\*\* \* Enter the full URL of your login page \* The sportsbook will redirect users automatically if they are not logged in \*\*Example\*\* \`\`\` https://partner-website.com/login || sendPostMessage \`\`\` \*\*Best Practices\*\* \* Ensure the login page supports redirect-back after successful login \* Use HTTPS only \*\*\* #### Deposit URL The \*\*Deposit URL\*\* specifies where users are redirected when they attempt to place a bet without sufficient balance. \*\*Purpose\*\* \* Seamless redirection to your payment or wallet system \* Improve conversion by guiding users directly to deposit \*\*Usage\*\* \* Enter the full URL of your deposit or cashier page \*\*Example\*\* \`\`\` https://partner-website.com/deposit \`\`\` \*\*When it is triggered\*\* \* User clicks “Place Bet” with insufficient balance \* User explicitly selects a deposit action inside the sportsbook \*\*\* #### Integration Sportsbook is available through the "game\\\_launch" API using the provider\\\_code and game\\\_code parameters. "provider\\\_code": "SPORTSBOOK"\\ "game\\\_code": "" --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents.md). # Onboarding Sub-Agents ## Sub-Agent Registration You are allowed to create one sub-agent per site. To initiate the process, click the \+ Create button located in the top-right corner of the page: \*\`https://{SERVER}/app/agent\`\*

| Field | Overview |
| --- | --- |
| Agent Code | A unique identifier used by the agent to log into the API panel. |
| Password | The agent’s login credentials. |
| Percent | Displays the revenue share for the parent agent’s account. |
| Memo | Reserved. |
| [Agent Type](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents.md#agent-type) | The dropdown menu provides various options. |
| [API Type](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents.md#api-type) | Specifies the method used for handling player funds and game sessions. |
| [Site Endpoint](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/onboarding-sub-agents.md#site-endpoint) | Defines the agent's backend callback URL used to handle requests from the `/gold_api` endpoint |

\### Agent Type \* \*\*Point Manager\*\*:\\ The Point Manager role is responsible for creating sub-agents and allocating API points to their downline. However, this role does not have access to operate or manage the casino site. \* \*\*Both Manager\*\*:\\ The Both Manager role combines the responsibilities of both the Point Manager and Site Manager. This role can create sub-agents, allocate API points to the downline, and manage casino site operations, offering full access to both agent network management and casino operations. \* \*\*Site Manager\*\*:\\ The Site Manager role is focused solely on operating the casino site. While this role can manage site operations, it does not have the ability to create sub-agents or manage the agent network. ### API Type \* \[\*\*Seamless\*\*\](/docs/getting-started/api-types/seamless.md)\\ The player’s balance remains in the casino wallet. The system communicates directly with the game provider during gameplay to update the balance in real-time without requiring manual transfers. \* Example: When a player starts a game, the balance is checked and updated directly from the casino wallet without moving funds. \* \[\*\*Transfer\*\*\](/docs/getting-started/api-types/transfer.md)\\ The player’s balance is transferred from the casino wallet to the game provider's wallet when the game starts, and any winnings or remaining balance are transferred back when the session ends. \* Example: When a player launches a slot game, 100 USDT is deducted from the casino wallet and moved to the game wallet. After the session, the remaining balance and winnings are transferred back to the casino wallet. ### Site Endpoint The Site Endpoint is used exclusively for the Seamless API. \\ This allows the system to communicate with the agent’s platform for tasks such as balance updates, game results, and transaction handling. \* Example: When a player completes a bet, the system sends a \`/gold\_api\` request to the specified Site Endpoint to update the player’s balance and record the game outcome. {% hint style="danger" %} When entering the Site Endpoint in profile page, ensure that it is an \`https\` domain and does not include \`/gold\_api\` at the end, as \`/gold\_api\` will be appended automatically when sending API requests. {% endhint %} Please note that the API panel is limited to creating sub-agents only. To create user accounts in Transfer mode, please utilize the \`user\_create\` API request. ## Deposit & Withdrawal To deposit or withdraw API balance to a sub-agent, click the Exchange button in the Action field.

![](https://1723341361-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%2Fuploads%2F4sV2qX13ZQDuYhR7Mnm0%2Fimage.png?alt=media&token=c4713ee7-1746-4e47-b14a-5ab18e7197e6)

Agent Manage

\## \*\*Agent Jackpot Config\*\* To set the Agent Jackpot, click the Config button in the Action field.

![](https://1723341361-files.gitbook.io/~/files/v0/b/gitbook-x-prod.appspot.com/o/spaces%2FGudIGoyLBIBfEiRAAJp7%2Fuploads%2FwbSD7myWlG32KCG6pLSX%2Fimage.png?alt=media&token=95c8b46c-a001-4a10-b832-eac48b43abf0)

Agent Manage

\## \*\*Deleting an Agent\*\* To delete an agent, click the last button in the Action field. {% hint style="warning" %} Before deleting a sub-agent, ensure that all their balance is transferred to your main account, \*as any remaining balance will be \*\*lost upon deletion\*\*.\* {% endhint %} --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. # Unknown

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/ip-whitelist.md). # IP Whitelist ## \*\*Overview\*\* The \*\*IP Whitelist\*\* is a security feature that ensures only authorized backend IP addresses are allowed to access the casino API. To ensure secure communication, agents must register their backend IP addresses into the IP Whitelist page. This process allows the \*\*Web Application Firewall (WAF)\*\* rule to validate and approve access to the API server from those specific IP addresses. \*\`https://{SERVER}/app/cloudflare\`\* ## \*\*How it Works:\*\* 1. \*\*Agent IP Registration\*\*:\\ Agents need to register their backend IP (either \*\*IPv4\*\* or \*\*IPv6\*\*) on the IP Whitelist page in the API panel to enable access to the casino API. 2. \*\*WAF Validation\*\*:\\ The \*\*Web Application Firewall (WAF)\*\* will verify the registered IP address. Once validated, the agent's backend can successfully communicate with the API server. 3. \*\*Duplicate IP Handling\*\*:\\ If an agent tries to register an IP address that is already whitelisted, an error message will appear indicating the IP is duplicated. In this case, no further registration is necessary. 4. \*\*IPv4/IPv6 Requirement\*\*:\\ It is mandatory that the agent's backend IP is either \*\*IPv4\*\* or \*\*IPv6\*\*. \\ Agents can obtain their server’s IP by running one the following commands in the CLI of their server system:  
\`\`\`bash curl ifconfig.me curl ipinfo.io/ip curl api.ipify.org curl icanhazip.com \`\`\` \\ This command will return the server’s \*\*IPv4\*\* or \*\*IPv6\*\* address, which should then be registered on the whitelist. --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/getting-started/back-office/call-manage.md). # Call Manage ## \*\*Overview:\*\* Free spins can be awarded to specific users, available only for slot games. Agents can select from the list of current players and grant them bonus spins through the following process. ## \*\*Accessing the List of Users:\*\* Agents can view the list of active players on the following page:\\ \*\`https://{SERVER}/app/call\`\* ## \*\*Important RTP Notes:\*\* \* When playing our games, users will always lose as part of the game’s design. \* If the \*\*target RTP\*\* is set at 95%, the user’s \*\*actual RTP\*\* will attempt to stay below this value, but never exceed it. \* To enhance the player experience, free spins can be provided to users via the API panel by you or site managers. \* The betting amount during a bonus call is factored into the RTP (Return to Player) calculations, ensuring that the overall RTP remains unaffected by the bonus call transactions. ## \*\*Granting Free Spins:\*\* 1. \*\*Via API access\*\*:\\ You can assign free spins directly to users from the site backend through the \*\*\`call\_apply\`\*\* API. 2. On the API Panel: \* Navigate to \*\*"Call & RTP -> Call Manage"\*\* to view the list of users. \* Select a free spin pattern from the available list and assign it to the desired user. \* \*\*Note:\*\* Call spins are only available after the user has made at least one spin. ## \*\*Types of Calls:\*\* \* \*\*Normal Call\*\*: Applies to regular user spins. \* \*\*Buy Call\*\*: Refers to bonus spins that a user purchases as part of a promotional feature (buying free spins). ## \*\*Free Spin Conditions:\*\* \* The \*\*Free Spin\*\* feature is only available after the user has completed at least one spin. \* If the user changes their bet amount after a free spin is granted, the free spin will be invalidated and fail to apply. \* Only the \*\*win amount\*\* can be defined when applying a free spin; the number of spins is fixed. --- # Agent Instructions This documentation is published with GitBook. # Game Launch | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch.md)
.

Launch Game[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#launch-game)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Launches a slot or live game and returns a URL for the player.

### Endpoint[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#endpoint)

Copy

    POST https://{API_SERVER}

**Content-Type:** `application/json`

### Request[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#request)

**Method:** `game_launch`

#### Slot game[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#slot-game)

Copy

    {
        "method": "game_launch",
        "agent_code": "your_agent_code",
        "agent_token": "your_agent_token",
        "user_code": "test",
        "provider_code": "PRAGMATIC",
        "game_code": "vs20doghouse",
        "lang": "en",
        "rtp": 92,
        "lobby_url": "https://your-site.com/lobby"
    }

#### Live game[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#live-game)

> For live games, `game_code` is optional. When empty, a lobby URL is returned.

Field

Type

Required

Description

`method`

string

Yes

Must be `game_launch`

`agent_code`

string

Yes

Your agent code

`agent_token`

string

Yes

Your agent API token

`user_code`

string

Yes

Player identifier

`provider_code`

string

Yes

Provider code

`game_code`

string

Optional

Game code (optional for live lobby)

`lang`

string

Yes

Language code (e.g. `en`)

`rtp`

number

No

Target RTP for this launch. If omitted, the user's configured RTP is used

`lobby_url`

string

No

URL to redirect the player when they exit the game. Defaults to empty string

### Response[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#response)

#### Success[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#success)

#### Failure[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-launch#failure)

[PreviousGame List](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list)
[NextAgent & User Info](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/agent-and-user-info)

# Game List | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list.md)
.

### Endpoint[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#endpoint)

Copy

    POST https://{API_SERVER}

**Content-Type:** `application/json`

### Request[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#request)

#### game\_list (v1)[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#game_list-v1)

**Method:** `game_list`

Copy

    {
        "method": "game_list",
        "agent_code": "your_agent_code",
        "agent_token": "your_agent_token",
        "provider_code": "PRAGMATIC"
    }

**Supported providers for** `**game_list**`**:**

Copy

    All providers

#### game\_list\_v2 (v2)[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#game_list_v2-v2)

**Method:** `game_list_v2`

Copy

    {
        "method": "game_list_v2",
        "agent_code": "your_agent_code",
        "agent_token": "your_agent_token",
        "provider_code": "PRAGMATIC"
    }

**Supported providers for** `**game_list_v2**`**:**

Field

Type

Required

Description

`method`

string

Yes

`game_list` or `game_list_v2`

`agent_code`

string

Yes

Your agent code

`agent_token`

string

Yes

Your agent API token

`provider_code`

string

Yes

Provider code

### Response[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#response)

#### Success — v1[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#success-v1)

#### Success — v2[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#success-v2)

#### `bet_levels`[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#bet_levels)

The bets the game offers, as `level number` → `stake of one spin`. Level `1` is always the smallest bet, and the levels go up from there.

Part

Meaning

Key

The bet level, counting from `1`

Value

What one spin at that level costs, in **your** currency

The values are worked out for the agent making the request, from your currency and your bet range, so two agents can get different numbers for the same game. How many levels a game has differs per game as well — read the list, don't assume a fixed length.

Use the key as the `bet_level` of **Create Free Tour**. With `"bet_levels": { "3": 0.75 }`, sending `"bet_level": 3` stakes each of that tour's free rounds at 0.75.

> `**PRAGMATIC**` **only for now.** `bet_levels` is the one provider that exposes its bet range so far. Every other provider leaves the field out of the game object entirely, so treat it as optional and do not build a UI that expects it everywhere. More providers are planned.

#### Failure[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list#failure)

When empty, a lobby URL is returned.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `method` | string | Yes | Must be `game_launch` |
| `agent_code` | string | Yes | Your agent code |
| `agent_token` | string | Yes | Your agent API token |
| `user_code` | string | Yes | Player identifier |
| `provider_code` | string | Yes | Provider code |
| `game_code` | string | Optional | Game code (optional for live lobby) |
| `lang` | string | Yes | Language code (e.g. `en`) |
| `rtp` | number | No  | Target RTP for this launch. If omitted, the user's configured RTP is used |
| `lobby_url` | string | No  | URL to redirect the player when they exit the game. Defaults to empty string |

# Seamless API | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api.md)
.

Your site must implement this callback APIs. It is **required** for Seamless mode only.

The platform sends requests to your configured site endpoint (e.g. `https://your-backend.com/gold_api`).

When entering the Site Endpoint in profile page, ensure that it is an `https` domain and does not include `/gold_api` at the end, as `/gold_api` will be appended automatically when sending API requests.

[PreviousTransfer Status](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/transfer-status)
[NextUser Balance (Site Endpoint)](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/user-balance-site-endpoint)

# Rate Limit | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/rate-limit.md)
.

The following APIs are subject to usage limits:

*   **user\_withdraw\_reset** (when "all\_users" is set to true): Once per minute
    
*   **get\_game\_log**: Once per 2 seconds
    
*   **game\_list**: Once per second
    
*   **provider\_list**: Once per second
    
*   **agent\_statistics**: Once every 5 minutes
    

[PreviousControl User RTP](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/rtp-api/control-user-rtp)

Required

# Cancel Tour | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour.md)
.

Ends a running free tour early. Use the `tour_id` you sent to **Create Free Tour**.

> `**PRAGMATIC**` **only for now**, like **Create Free Tour** — there are no tours on other providers to cancel.

### Endpoint[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#endpoint)

Copy

    POST https://{API_SERVER}

**Content-Type:** `application/json`

### Request[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#request)

**Method:** `tour_cancel`

Copy

    {
        "method": "tour_cancel",
        "agent_code": "your_agent_code",
        "agent_token": "your_agent_token",
        "tour_id": "free_tour_777"
    }

Field

Type

Required

Description

`method`

string

Yes

Must be `tour_cancel`

`agent_code`

string

Yes

Your agent code

`agent_token`

string

Yes

Your agent API token

`tour_id`

string

Yes

The `tour_id` of the tour to cancel

### Response[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#response)

#### Success[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#success)

Field

Type

Description

`rest_count`

integer

Free rounds the user never got to play

`total_win`

number

What the tour had already paid the user

`canceled_money`

number

Returned to your agent balance: `real_win` − `total_win`

The user keeps what the tour already paid. Only the part that was never played is given back.

#### Failure[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#failure)

`detail`

Description

`TOUR_NOT_FOUND`

The game server has no such tour, or it already ended

`TOUR_IN_PROGRESS`

A free round is being played right now. Cancelling mid-round would turn it into a paid one — retry once the round is over

A tour can only be canceled once. `There is no running tour.` also comes back for a tour that was already canceled.

[PreviousCreate Tour](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour)
[NextRTP API](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/rtp-api)

Last updated 16 days ago

Was this helpful?

*   [Endpoint](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#endpoint)
    
*   [Request](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#request)
    
*   [Response](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour#response)
    

Was this helpful?

Copy

    {
        "status": 1,
        "tour_id": "free_tour_777",
        "user_code": "test",
        "provider_code": "PRAGMATIC",
        "game_code": "vs20doghouse",
        "rest_count": 6,
        "total_win": 21400,
        "canceled_money": 42950,
        "msg": "SUCCESS"
    }

Copy

        "status": 0,
        "msg": "There is no running tour."
    }

Copy

        "status": 0,
        "msg": "External Error",
        "detail": "TOUR_IN_PROGRESS"
    }

        },
        "user": {

# In-Game History | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/in-game-history.md)

Unlike `get_game_log`, this does not return transaction records directly — it returns a link to the provider's history page.

> **Availability:** Currently supported for **Pragmatic Play Slot** only (`provider_code`: `PRAGMATIC`). Other providers will return `INVALID_PROVIDER`.

# Create Tour | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour.md)
.

Grants a user a bundle of free rounds (a "free tour") on one slot game. The whole tour is laid out in advance when it is created, so the response already tells you what it will pay.

Only one tour can run per user and game at a time — the previous one has to finish, expire or be canceled first.

> `**PRAGMATIC**` **only for now.** Free tours are supported on Pragmatic games and nowhere else yet. Sending `tour_create` for any other `provider_code` fails, since that provider's game server has no tour to create. More providers are planned.

### Endpoint[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour#endpoint)

Copy

    POST https://{API_SERVER}

**Content-Type:** `application/json`

### Request[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour#request)

**Method:** `tour_create`

Copy

    {
        "method": "tour_create",
        "agent_code": "your_agent_code",
        "agent_token": "your_agent_token",
        "user_code": "test",
        "provider_code": "PRAGMATIC",
        "game_code": "vs20doghouse",
        "bet_level": 1,
        "spin_count": 10,
        "amount": 70000,
        "expiration_time": "2026-02-26T06:46:04.000Z",
        "tour_id": "free_tour_777"
    }

Field

Type

Required

Description

`method`

string

Yes

Must be `tour_create`

`agent_code`

string

Yes

Your agent code

`agent_token`

string

Yes

Your agent API token

`user_code`

string

Yes

Target user

`provider_code`

string

Yes

Game provider code

`game_code`

string

Yes

Game identifier

`bet_level`

integer

Yes

Index into the game's bet range: `1` = minimum bet, `2` = the next one, and so on

`spin_count`

integer

Yes

Number of free rounds. `1` – `1000`

`amount`

number

Yes

Max win of the whole tour, in your currency. At least the equivalent of 7 USD at your currency rate

`expiration_time`

datetime

Yes

When the tour stops being playable. Must be in the future

`tour_id`

string

Yes

Your own identifier for the tour, 1 – 36 characters. It comes back in the later callbacks

> `**amount**` **is a ceiling, not a target.** The tour is shaped like an ordinary session — only a few of its rounds pay — so what it really pays out is usually below `amount`. That figure comes back as `real_win`.

### Response[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour#response)

#### Success[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour#success)

Field

Type

Description

`bet`

number

Coin bet of one line at `bet_level`

`total_bet`

number

Bet of one free round (`bet` × the game's pay lines)

`real_win`

number

What the prepared rounds actually pay, up to `amount`

**Balance:** `real_win` is deducted from your agent balance the moment the tour is created, and the unplayed part comes back if you cancel it. Your balance still has to cover the full `amount` at creation time, before `real_win` is known.

#### Failure[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour#failure)

`External Error` means the game server refused the tour. `detail` says why:

`detail`

Description

`INVALID_GAME`

The game does not exist or has no pay lines

`PO_GAME`

The game is switched off

`INVALID_BET_LEVEL`

`bet_level` is above the game's highest bet

`INVALID_SPIN_COUNT`

`spin_count` is outside `1` – `1000`

`INVALID_AMOUNT`

`amount` is below the 7 USD equivalent for your currency

`INVALID_EXPIRATION_TIME`

`expiration_time` is missing or already past

`INVALID_TOUR_ID`

`tour_id` is longer than 36 characters

`DUPLICATED_TOUR_ID`

The game server already knows this `tour_id`

`TOUR_IN_PROGRESS`

This user still has a playable tour on this game

`NO_PATTERN_DATA`

The game has no round data to build a tour from

A refused tour is not recorded, so the same `tour_id` can be used again once the cause is fixed.

[PreviousFreeSpin Tour API](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api)
[NextCancel Tour](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/cancel-tour)

Last updated 16 days ago

Was this helpful?

*   [Request](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour#request)
    
*   [Response](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api/create-tour#response)

        "status": 1,

# Deposit / Withdraw User Balance | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/deposit-withdraw-user-balance.md)
.

End**point**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/deposit-withdraw-user-balance#endpoint)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy

    POST https://{API_SERVER}

**Request Body**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/deposit-withdraw-user-balance#request-body)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy

    {
        "method": "user_deposit",
        "agent_code": "testAgent",
        "agent_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
        "user_code": "test",
        "amount": 10000.00,
        "agent_sign"："UNIQUE_ID"   // Optional to prevent duplicated
    }

Copy

    {
        "method": "user_withdraw",
        "agent_code": "testAgent",
        "agent_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
        "user_code": "test",
        "amount": 10000.00,
        "agent_sign"："UNIQUE_ID"   // Optional to prevent duplicated
    }

Key

Type

Description

`method`

string

API method name. For balance transfer to a user game wallet, the value must be `user_deposit` or `user_withdraw`.

`agent_code`

string

Unique agent identifier provided by NEXUS. Used to identify which agent is sending the request.

`agent_token`

string

Secret authentication token assigned to the agent. Used for API authentication and request validation.

`user_code`

string

Unique user identifier within the agent’s platform. This user will receive the transferred balance.

`amount`

double

Amount to transfer from the agent balance to the user API balance. Supports up to 2 digits after the decimal point. Example: `10000.00`

`agent_sign`

string

Optional unique request identifier used to prevent duplicated requests. Recommended for transaction safety and idempotency handling. Example: UUID, hash string, timestamp-based unique value.

**Response Example**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/deposit-withdraw-user-balance#response-example)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Field

Type

Description

`status`

int

Request processing status. `1` indicates success, while `0` indicates failure.

`msg`

string

Response message describing the result of the request. Example: `SUCCESS: "INSUFFICIENT_AGENT_FUNDS"`.

`agent_balance`

double

Remaining balance of the agent after the transaction has been processed successfully. Supports up to 2 digits after the decimal point.

`user_balance`

double

Updated API balance of the user after the transaction has been completed successfully. Supports up to 2 digits after the decimal point.

[PreviousTransfer API](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api)
[NextReset User Balance](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/reset-user-balance)

Last updated 2 months ago

*   [Response Example](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/deposit-withdraw-user-balance#response-example)

        "status": 1, 
        "msg": "SUCCESS",
        "agent_balance": 990000,
        "user_balance": 10000
