# Transaction (Site Endpoint) | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint.md)
.

Endpoint[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#endpoint)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy

    POST https://{YOUR_SITE}/gold_api

**Content-Type:** `application/json`

**Method:** `transaction`

Request[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#request)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### Slot transaction[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#slot-transaction)

Copy

    {
        "method": "transaction",
        "agent_code": "your_agent_code",
        "agent_secret": "your_agent_secret",
        "agent_balance": 10000000,
        "user_code": "test",
        "user_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
        "user_balance": 99200,
        "game_type": "slot",
        "slot": {
            "provider_code": "PRAGMATIC",
            "game_code": "vs20midas",
            "type": "BASE",
            "bet_money": 1000,
            "win_money": 200,
            "round_id": 63792613432127,
            "txn_id": "63792613432127",
            "txn_id_v2": "64a83f2fc597acc9004eec52c3f84c30",
            "txn_type": "debit_credit"
        }
    }

### Live transaction[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#live-transaction)

### Sportsbook transaction[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#sportsbook-transaction)

### Mini game (MN) transaction[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#mini-game-mn-transaction)

### Game object fields[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#game-object-fields)

**Important:** The game detail object key **must match** the `game_type` value.

> Example: if `"game_type": "slot"`, the payload must include a `"slot": { ... }` object. If `"game_type": "SB"`, use `"SB": { ... }`.

game\_type

Provider Type

`slot`

Slot

`live`

Live Casino

`SB`

Sportsbook

`MN`

Mini Game

`FT`

Fish

Fields inside the game object (`slot` / `live` / `SB` / `MN`):

Field

Type

Required

Description

`provider_code`

string

Yes

Game provider code

`game_code`

string

Yes

Game identifier

`type`

string

Yes

Bet/round type (e.g. `BASE`, `baccarat`)

`bet_money`

number

Yes

Bet amount

`win_money`

number

Yes

Win amount

`round_id`

integer

Yes

Unique ID for one bet. Every transaction the bet produces shares it

`txn_id`

string

Yes

The `round_id` as a string.

Starting October 15, 2026 (UTC): `txn_id` will use the same value as `txn_id_v2`.

`txn_id_v2`

string

Yes

Unique per inner spin of one bet. Deduplicate on this field. `txn_id_v2` will be deprecated on October 31, 2026 (UTC).

`txn_type`

string

Yes

`debit`, `credit`, or `debit_credit`

> `**round_id**` **and** `**txn_id_v2**`
> 
> One bet is not always one payout. A bet can play out over several **inner spins** — tumbling wins, free game rounds, the closing collect — and every one of them is sent as its own transaction.
> 
> *   `round_id` — one bet (integer). Every transaction that bet produces carries the same value.
>     
> *   `txn_id_v2` — unique per inner spin (string). No two transactions ever share it.
>     
> 
> **Deduplicate on** `**txn_id_v2**`**.** `round_id` is unique per _bet_, not per transaction, so treating it as an idempotency key makes your site drop the win of a round it already booked the bet for.

### Live / Sportsbook `info` field[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#live-sportsbook-info-field)

The `info` field (top-level, for live and sportsbook) is a **stringified JSON** object with bet/result details.

**Sportsbook** `**info**` **fields:**

Field

Description

`couponCode`

Bet coupon code

`status`

`pending`, `won`, `lost`, `cashedout`, or `canceled`

`totalOdds`

Combined odds

`potentialWin`

Potential payout

`stake`

Bet stake

`payout`

Actual payout

`betslips`

Array of `{ id, sportName, countryName, leagueName, matchName, homeTeam, awayTeam, marketName, oddName, oddRate, oddPoint }`

`settlementData`

Settlement details object

Transaction Type Reference[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#transaction-type-reference)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

`txn_type` values: `debit`, `credit`, `debit_credit`

Type

Description

`bet_money`

`win_money`

`debit`

Only send bet amount

Valid

Invalid (don't use)

`credit`

Only send win amount

Invalid (don't use)

Valid

`debit_credit`

Send bet and win amount

Valid

Valid

### Txn examples[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#txn-examples)

One bet (`round_id`, and the `txn_id` mirroring it) can produce several transactions, each with its own `txn_id_v2`.

Response[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#response)

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Must return the updated user balance:

### Failure[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/transaction-site-endpoint#failure)

[PreviousUser Balance (Site Endpoint)](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/user-balance-site-endpoint)
[NextCALL API](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api)

# Call History | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history.md)
.

### Endpoint[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#endpoint)

Copy

    POST https://{API_SERVER}

**Content-Type:** `application/json`

### Request[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#request)

**Method:** `call_history`

Copy

    {
        "method": "call_history",
        "agent_code": "your_agent_code",
        "agent_token": "your_agent_token",
        "offset": 0,
        "limit": 100
    }

Field

Type

Required

Description

`method`

string

Yes

Must be `call_history`

`agent_code`

string

Yes

Your agent code

`agent_token`

string

Yes

Your agent API token

`offset`

number

Yes

Pagination offset (starting index)

`limit`

number

Yes

Number of records to return

### Response[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#response)

#### Success[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#success)

#### Failure[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#failure)

### Call Status Reference[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#call-status-reference)

Status

Meaning

`0`

Waiting

`1`

Processing

`2`

Finished

`3`

Rejected by game server (bet amount changed)

`4`

Canceled (via Cancel API or back-office button)

### Expect, Missed, and Real Money[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#expect-missed-and-real-money)

Field

Description

`expect`

Amount reserved when the admin applies the call (e.g. 1000)

`real`

Amount the user actually received (e.g. 800)

`missed`

Difference returned to the admin (`expect − real`, e.g. 200)

#### Why missed money?[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#why-missed-money)

Some Pragmatic Play games with free-option mechanics (e.g. 5 Lions Megaways, The Dog House Megaways, Wild Booster) may not deliver the full expected payout. Unused funds are returned to the agent.

### Status Examples[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#status-examples)

#### Waiting (`status: 0`)[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#waiting-status-0)

#### Processing (`status: 1`)[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#processing-status-1)

#### Finished (`status: 2`)[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#finished-status-2)

#### Rejected — bet changed (`status: 3`)[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#rejected-bet-changed-status-3)

#### Canceled (`status: 4`)[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history#canceled-status-4)

[PreviousCall Cancel](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-cancel)
[NextFreeSpin Tour API](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/freespin-tour-api)

# Game Log | Casino API

For the complete documentation index, see [llms.txt](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt)
. This page is also available as [Markdown](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log.md)
.

End**point**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log#endpoint)

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy

    POST https://{API_SERVER}

**Request Body**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log#request-body)

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Copy

    {
      "method": "get_game_log",
      "agent_code": "testAgent",
      "agent_token": "41f5cc794aef2b138c715cf25f767cc1",
      "user_code": "tsetAgent",
      "game_type": "slot",
      "start": "2021-09-17 00:00:00",
      "end": "2021-09-17 23:59:00",
      "page": 0,
      "perPage": 1000
    }

Key

Type

Description

**method**

string

The method type(`get_game_log`)

**agent\_code**

string

The agent's code

**agent\_token**

string

The agent's authentication token

**user\_code**

string

The user's code

**game\_type**

string

The type of game for which the log is being retrieved.

**start**

string

The start date and time for the search in the format `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss`.

**end**

string

The end date and time for the search in the format `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss`.

**page**

integer

The page number for pagination (used for multiple results).

**perPage**

integer

The number of records to return per page (maximum 1,000,000).

**Success Response**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log#success-response)

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Key

Type

Description

**status**

integer (32-bit)

The response status. `1` means success.

**total\_count**

integer (32-bit)

The total number of logs that match the query criteria.

**page**

integer (32-bit)

The current page number of the results.

**perPage**

integer (32-bit)

The number of records returned per page (as per the request).

**slot**

array

A list of game transaction logs matching the request, with each log entry containing transaction details.

**Transaction Log Details (Inside** `**slot**` **array):**

Key

Type

Description

**history\_id**

integer (64-bit)

The unique ID of the transaction history entry.

**agent\_code**

string

The code of the agent who processed the transaction.

**user\_code**

string

The code of the user who made the transaction.

**provider\_code**

string

The code of the game provider.

**game\_code**

string

The unique game identifier (e.g., `vs20doghouse`).

**type**

string

The type of transaction (e.g., `BASE`, `FREE`).

**bet\_money**

double

The amount of money bet by the user.

**win\_money**

double

The amount of money won by the user.

**txn\_id**

string

The unique transaction ID.

**txn\_type**

string

The type of transaction (e.g., `debit_credit`)

**user\_start\_balance**

double

The user's balance before the transaction.

**user\_end\_balance**

double

The user's balance after the transaction.

**agent\_start\_balance**

double

The agent's balance before the transaction.

**agent\_end\_balance**

double

The agent's balance after the transaction.

**created\_at**

string

The date and time when the transaction was created.

### **Example Success Response**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log#example-success-response)

### **Failure Response Example**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log#failure-response-example)

#### **Explanation:**[](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log#explanation)

Key

Type

Description

**status**

integer (32-bit)

Indicates the response status. A value of `0` indicates a failure.

**msg**

string

A brief message describing the error. In this case, `Invalid Parameter.` indicates an invalid parameter was provided.

**detail**

string

A more detailed message explaining the specific issue.

[NextIn-Game History](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/in-game-history)

`call_rtp`

number

Yes

RTP value from **Get Call List**

`call_type`

number

Yes

`1` = Common Free, `2` = Buy Bonus Free

- \[FastSpin\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/appendix/languages/fastspin.md)
- \[Joker Gaming\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/appendix/languages/joker-gaming.md): Most games are primarily built in English, with some supporting multiple languages.

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-list.md).

# Game List

This is an API endpoint for retrieving a list of games associated with a specific provider.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

#### game\\\_list (v1)

\*\*Method:\*\* \`game\_list\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "game\_list",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC"
}
\`\`\`

\*\*Supported providers for \`game\_list\`:\*\*

\`\`\`
All providers
\`\`\`

#### game\\\_list\\\_v2 (v2)

\*\*Method:\*\* \`game\_list\_v2\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "game\_list\_v2",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC"
}
\`\`\`

\*\*Supported providers for \`game\_list\_v2\`:\*\*

\`\`\`
PRAGMATIC, PGSOFT, REELKINGDOM, FATPANDA, HABANERO, CQ9
\`\`\`

| Field           | Type   | Required | Description                   |
| --------------- | ------ | -------- | ----------------------------- |
| \`method\`        | string | Yes      | \`game\_list\` or \`game\_list\_v2\` |
| \`agent\_code\`    | string | Yes      | Your agent code               |
| \`agent\_token\`   | string | Yes      | Your agent API token          |
| \`provider\_code\` | string | Yes      | Provider code                 |

### Response

#### Success — v1

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "games": \[\
        {\
            "game\_code": "vs243mwarrior",\
            "game\_name": "Monkey Warrior",\
            "banner": "https://.../vs243mwarrior.png",\
            "status": 1,\
            "bet\_levels": {\
                "1": 0.25,\
                "2": 0.5,\
                "3": 0.75,\
                "4": 1,\
                "5": 2.5,\
                "6": 5,\
                "7": 12.5,\
                "8": 25,\
                "9": 50,\
                "10": 125\
            }\
        }\
    \]
}
\`\`\`

#### Success — v2

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "games": \[\
        {\
            "id": 1,\
            "game\_code": "vs243mwarrior",\
            "game\_name": {\
                "hi": "मंकी वारियर™",\
                "vi": "Chiến Binh Khỉ",\
                "ko": "몽키 워리어",\
                "th": "นักรบลิง",\
                "zh-hant": "酷猴戰士",\
                "zh-hans": "酷猴战士"\
            },\
            "bet\_levels": {\
                "1": 0.25,\
                "2": 0.5,\
                "3": 0.75,\
                "4": 1,\
                "5": 2.5,\
                "6": 5,\
                "7": 12.5,\
                "8": 25,\
                "9": 50,\
                "10": 125\
            }\
        }\
    \]
}
\`\`\`

#### \`bet\_levels\`

The bets the game offers, as \`level number\` → \`stake of one spin\`. Level \`1\` is always the smallest bet, and the levels go up from there.

| Part  | Meaning                                                 |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| Key   | The bet level, counting from \`1\`                        |
| Value | What one spin at that level costs, in \*\*your\*\* currency |

The values are worked out for the agent making the request, from your currency and your bet range, so two agents can get different numbers for the same game. How many levels a game has differs per game as well — read the list, don't assume a fixed length.

Use the key as the \`bet\_level\` of \*\*Create Free Tour\*\*. With \`"bet\_levels": { "3": 0.75 }\`, sending \`"bet\_level": 3\` stakes each of that tour's free rounds at 0.75.

> \*\*\`PRAGMATIC\` only for now.\*\* \`bet\_levels\` is the one provider that exposes its bet range so far. Every other provider leaves the field out of the game object entirely, so treat it as optional and do not build a UI that expects it everywhere. More providers are planned.

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "INTERNAL\_ERROR"
}
\`\`\`

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-apply.md).

# Call Apply

Applies a call (bonus) to a user in a specific game. Use call\\\_rtp from the Get Call List response.

## Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`call\_apply\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "call\_apply",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
    "game\_code": "vs20doghouse",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "call\_rtp": 45010,
    "call\_type": 1
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="156">Field</th><th width="113">Type</th><th width="85">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>call\_apply</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>provider\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game provider code</td></tr><tr><td><code>game\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game identifier</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Target user</td></tr><tr><td><code>call\_rtp</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>RTP value from <strong>Get Call List</strong></td></tr><tr><td><code>call\_type</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td><code>1</code> = Common Free, <code>2</code> = Buy Bonus Free</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "called\_money": 720160,
    "msg": "SUCCESS"
}
\`\`\`

\`called\_money\` is calculated as:

\`\`\`
called\_money = bet × (rtp / 100)
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "Processing call is existed."
}
\`\`\`


---

# Seamless API

{% hint style="warning" %}
Your site must implement this callback APIs. It is \*\*required\*\* for Seamless mode only.
{% endhint %}

The platform sends requests to your configured site endpoint (e.g. \`https://your-backend.com/gold\_api\`).

{% hint style="danger" %}
When entering the Site Endpoint in profile page, ensure that it is an \`https\` domain and does not include \`/gold\_api\` at the end, as \`/gold\_api\` will be appended automatically when sending API requests.
{% endhint %}

# Current Playing Users

Returns users currently playing slot games under your agent.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`call\_players\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "call\_players",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token"
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="138">Field</th><th width="115">Type</th><th width="108">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>call\_players</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "data": \[\
        {\
            "user\_code": "test",\
            "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",\
            "game\_code": "vs20doghouse",\
            "bet": 1600,\
            "balance": 11804848.2,\
            "total\_debit": 1271837.75,\
            "total\_credit": 3383894.16,\
            "target\_rtp": 92,\
            "real\_rtp": 266.06\
        }\
    \]
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="168">Field</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>User identifier</td></tr><tr><td><code>provider\_code</code></td><td>Game provider code</td></tr><tr><td><code>game\_code</code></td><td>Game identifier</td></tr><tr><td><code>bet</code></td><td>Current bet amount</td></tr><tr><td><code>balance</code></td><td>User balance</td></tr><tr><td><code>total\_debit</code></td><td>Total debited amount</td></tr><tr><td><code>total\_credit</code></td><td>Total credited amount</td></tr><tr><td><code>target\_rtp</code></td><td>Target RTP for the user</td></tr><tr><td><code>real\_rtp</code></td><td>Actual RTP for the user</td></tr></tbody></table>

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "Invalid Agent."
}
\`\`\`

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/game-api/game-log.md).

# Game Log

This is an API used to retrieve the game log for a specific user or agent, including transaction details for a specified time range.

## End\*\*point\*\*

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

## \*\*Request Body\*\*

\`\`\`json
{
  "method": "get\_game\_log",
  "agent\_code": "testAgent",
  "agent\_token": "41f5cc794aef2b138c715cf25f767cc1",
  "user\_code": "tsetAgent",
  "game\_type": "slot",
  "start": "2021-09-17 00:00:00",
  "end": "2021-09-17 23:59:00",
  "page": 0,
  "perPage": 1000
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="128">Key</th><th width="89">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>method</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The method type(<code>get\_game\_log</code>) </td></tr><tr><td><strong>agent\_code</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The agent's code</td></tr><tr><td><strong>agent\_token</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The agent's authentication token</td></tr><tr><td><a data-footnote-ref href="#user-content-fn-1"><strong>user\_code</strong></a></td><td>string</td><td>The user's code</td></tr><tr><td><a data-footnote-ref href="#user-content-fn-2"><strong>game\_type</strong></a></td><td>string</td><td>The type of game for which the log is being retrieved.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>start</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The start date and time for the search in the format <code>yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>end</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The end date and time for the search in the format <code>yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>page</strong></td><td>integer</td><td>The page number for pagination (used for multiple results).</td></tr><tr><td><strong>perPage</strong></td><td>integer</td><td>The number of records to return per page (maximum 1,000,000).</td></tr></tbody></table>

## \*\*Success Response\*\*

<table><thead><tr><th width="132">Key</th><th width="139">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>status</strong></td><td>integer (32-bit)</td><td>The response status. <code>1</code> means success.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>total\_count</strong></td><td>integer (32-bit)</td><td>The total number of logs that match the query criteria.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>page</strong></td><td>integer (32-bit)</td><td>The current page number of the results.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>perPage</strong></td><td>integer (32-bit)</td><td>The number of records returned per page (as per the request).</td></tr><tr><td><strong>slot</strong></td><td>array</td><td>A list of game transaction logs matching the request, with each log entry containing transaction details.</td></tr></tbody></table>

\*\*Transaction Log Details (Inside \`slot\` array):\*\*

<table><thead><tr><th width="185">Key</th><th width="144">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>history\_id</strong></td><td>integer (64-bit)</td><td>The unique ID of the transaction history entry.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>agent\_code</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The code of the agent who processed the transaction.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>user\_code</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The code of the user who made the transaction.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>provider\_code</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The code of the game provider.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>game\_code</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The unique game identifier (e.g., <code>vs20doghouse</code>).</td></tr><tr><td><strong>type</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The type of transaction (e.g., <code>BASE</code>, <code>FREE</code>).</td></tr><tr><td><strong>bet\_money</strong></td><td>double</td><td>The amount of money bet by the user.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>win\_money</strong></td><td>double</td><td>The amount of money won by the user.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>txn\_id</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The unique transaction ID.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>txn\_type</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The type of transaction (e.g., <code>debit\_credit</code>)</td></tr><tr><td><strong>user\_start\_balance</strong></td><td>double</td><td>The user's balance before the transaction.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>user\_end\_balance</strong></td><td>double</td><td>The user's balance after the transaction.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>agent\_start\_balance</strong></td><td>double</td><td>The agent's balance before the transaction.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>agent\_end\_balance</strong></td><td>double</td><td>The agent's balance after the transaction.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>created\_at</strong></td><td>string</td><td>The date and time when the transaction was created.</td></tr></tbody></table>

### \*\*Example Success Response\*\*

\`\`\`json
{
  "status": 1,
  "total\_count": 340,
  "page": 0,
  "perPage": 1000,
  "slot": \[\
    {\
      "history\_id": 777,\
      "agent\_code": "admin",\
      "user\_code": "test",\
      "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",\
      "game\_code": "vs20doghouse",\
      "type": "BASE",\
      "bet\_money": 2000,\
      "win\_money": 0,\
      "txn\_id": "64a83f2fc597acc9004eec52c3f84c30",\
      "txn\_type": "debit\_credit",\
      "user\_start\_balance": 230500,\
      "user\_end\_balance": 228500,\
      "agent\_start\_balance": 22092000,\
      "agent\_end\_balance": 22092000,\
      "created\_at": "2021-09-17T12:50:42.000Z"\
    },\
    ...\
  \]
}
\`\`\`

### \*\*Failure Response Example\*\*

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "Invalid Parameter.",
    "detail": "perPage must be less than or equal to 1000000"
}
\`\`\`

#### \*\*Explanation:\*\*

<table><thead><tr><th width="97">Key</th><th width="141">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>status</strong></td><td>integer (32-bit)</td><td>Indicates the response status. A value of <code>0</code> indicates a failure.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>msg</strong></td><td>string</td><td>A brief message describing the error. In this case, <code>Invalid Parameter.</code> indicates an invalid parameter was provided.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>detail</strong></td><td>string</td><td>A more detailed message explaining the specific issue. </td></tr></tbody></table>

\[^1\]: If not specified, the API will return the entire transaction history for the agent.

\[^2\]: \`slot\` for slot games, \`live\` for live casino

# RTP API

- \[Control User RTP\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/rtp-api/control-user-rtp.md): Sets the target RTP for a specific user on a provider.

# Rate Limit

The following APIs are subject to usage limits:

\* \*\*user\\\_withdraw\\\_reset\*\* (when "all\\\_users" is set to true): Once per minute
\* \*\*get\\\_game\\\_log\*\*: Once per 2 seconds
\* \*\*game\\\_list\*\*: Once per second
\* \*\*provider\\\_list\*\*: Once per second
\* \*\*agent\\\_statistics\*\*: Once every 5 minutes

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/seamless-api/user-balance-site-endpoint.md).

# User Balance (Site Endpoint)

Called to retrieve the current balance for a user before or during gameplay.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{YOUR\_SITE}/gold\_api
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

\*\*Method:\*\* \`user\_balance\`

### Request

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "user\_balance",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_secret": "your\_agent\_secret",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "user\_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
    "game\_code": "vs20olympgate"
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="132">Field</th><th width="104">Type</th><th width="110">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>user\_balance</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_secret</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Agent secret key</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>User identifier</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>User session token</td></tr><tr><td><code>game\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game being played</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

Return balance even if it is \`0\`:

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "user\_balance": 0
}
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "user\_balance": 0,
    "msg": "INTERNAL\_ERROR"
}
\`\`\`


---

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/rtp-api/control-users-rtp.md).

# Control Users RTP

Sets the target RTP for multiple users in one request.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`control\_users\_rtp\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "control\_users\_rtp",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "user\_codes": "\[\\"test1\\",\\"test2\\"\]",
    "rtp": 92
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="127">Field</th><th width="121">Type</th><th width="114">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>control\_users\_rtp</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_codes</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Stringified JSON array of user codes</td></tr><tr><td><code>rtp</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>Target RTP value (0 ~ 999)</td></tr></tbody></table>

> \`user\_codes\` must be a \*\*stringified JSON array\*\*, not a native JSON array.

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "changed\_rtp": 92
}
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "Invalid JSON"
}
\`\`\`

# Transfer Status

Checks the status of a previous deposit or withdraw transaction by \\\`agent\\\_sign\\\`.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`transfer\_status\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "transfer\_status",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "agent\_sign": "UNIQUE\_ID"
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="122">Field</th><th width="101">Type</th><th width="109">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>transfer\_status</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Target user</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_sign</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Unique ID from the original deposit/withdraw request</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "amount": 10000,
    "agent\_balance": 990000,
    "user\_balance": 10000,
    "type": "user\_deposit"
}
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "NOT FOUND"
}
\`\`\`

# Reset User Balance

Withdraws the full balance from a user (or all users) and returns it to the agent. Used in Transfer mode.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`user\_withdraw\_reset\`

#### Single user

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "user\_withdraw\_reset",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "user\_code": "test"
}
\`\`\`

#### All users

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "user\_withdraw\_reset",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "all\_users": true
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="122">Field</th><th width="98">Type</th><th width="119">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>user\_withdraw\_reset</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Conditional</td><td>Required when resetting a single user</td></tr><tr><td><code>all\_users</code></td><td>boolean</td><td>No</td><td>When <code>true</code>, resets all users</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success — single user

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "agent": {
        "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
        "balance": 1000000
    },
    "user": {
        "user\_code": "test",
        "withdraw\_amount": 5600,
        "balance": 0
    }
}
\`\`\`

#### Success — all users

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "agent": {
        "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
        "balance": 1076850
    },
    "user\_list": \[\
        {\
            "user\_code": "test",\
            "withdraw\_amount": 20000,\
            "balance": 0\
        },\
        {\
            "user\_code": "test2",\
            "withdraw\_amount": 56850,\
            "balance": 0\
        }\
    \]
}
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "INVALID\_PARAMETER"
}
\`\`\`

# Cancel Tour

Ends a running free tour early. Use the \`tour\_id\` you sent to \*\*Create Free Tour\*\*.

> \*\*\`PRAGMATIC\` only for now\*\*, like \*\*Create Free Tour\*\* — there are no tours on other providers to cancel.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`tour\_cancel\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "tour\_cancel",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "tour\_id": "free\_tour\_777"
}
\`\`\`

| Field         | Type   | Required | Description                         |
| ------------- | ------ | -------- | ----------------------------------- |
| \`method\`      | string | Yes      | Must be \`tour\_cancel\`               |
| \`agent\_code\`  | string | Yes      | Your agent code                     |
| \`agent\_token\` | string | Yes      | Your agent API token                |
| \`tour\_id\`     | string | Yes      | The \`tour\_id\` of the tour to cancel |

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "tour\_id": "free\_tour\_777",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
    "game\_code": "vs20doghouse",
    "rest\_count": 6,
    "total\_win": 21400,
    "canceled\_money": 42950,
    "msg": "SUCCESS"
}
\`\`\`

| Field            | Type    | Description                                              |
| ---------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`rest\_count\`     | integer | Free rounds the user never got to play                   |
| \`total\_win\`      | number  | What the tour had already paid the user                  |
| \`canceled\_money\` | number  | Returned to your agent balance: \`real\_win\` − \`total\_win\` |

The user keeps what the tour already paid. Only the part that was never played is given back.

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "There is no running tour."
}
\`\`\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "External Error",
    "detail": "TOUR\_IN\_PROGRESS"
}
\`\`\`

| \`detail\`           | Description                                                                                                               |
| ------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`TOUR\_NOT\_FOUND\`   | The game server has no such tour, or it already ended                                                                     |
| \`TOUR\_IN\_PROGRESS\` | A free round is being played right now. Cancelling mid-round would turn it into a paid one — retry once the round is over |

A tour can only be canceled once. \`There is no running tour.\` also comes back for a tour that was already canceled.

# Agent & User Info

This is an API endpoint used to retrieve the balance information for a specific agent.

## \*\*Endpoint\*\*

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

### \*\*Request Body\*\*

{% code fullWidth="false" %}

  "method": "money\_info",
  "agent\_code": "testAgent",
  "agent\_token": "41f5cc794aef2b138c715cf25f767cc1"
}
\`\`\`

{% endcode %}

<table><thead><tr><th width="183">Key</th><th width="145">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>method</td><td>string</td><td>The method type (<code>money\_info</code>)</td></tr><tr><td>agent\_code</td><td>string</td><td>The agent's code</td></tr><tr><td>agent\_token</td><td>string</td><td>The agent's authentication token</td></tr></tbody></table>

    "status": 1,
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "agent": {
        "agent\_code": "testAgent",

# Control User RTP

Sets the target RTP for a specific user on a provider.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`control\_rtp\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "control\_rtp",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "rtp": 92
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="151">Field</th><th width="116">Type</th><th width="117">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>control\_rtp</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>provider\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game provider code</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Target user</td></tr><tr><td><code>rtp</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>Target RTP value (0 ~ 999)</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "changed\_rtp": 92
}
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "Invalid Parameter.",
    "detail": "rtp must be less than or equal to 999"
}
\`\`\`

# CALL API

- \[Current Playing Users\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/current-playing-users.md): Returns users currently playing slot games under your agent.
- \[Call List\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-list.md): Returns available call (free spin) options for a specific provider and game.
- \[Call Apply\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-apply.md): Applies a call (bonus) to a user in a specific game. Use call\\\_rtp from the Get Call List response.
- \[Call Cancel\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-cancel.md): Cancels a waiting call. Use call\\\_id from the Get Call History response.
- \[Call History\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history.md): Returns paginated call history for your agent.

# Call List

Returns available call (free spin) options for a specific provider and game.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`call\_list\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "call\_list",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
    "game\_code": "vs20doghouse"
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="139">Field</th><th width="109">Type</th><th width="110">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>call\_list</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>provider\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game provider code</td></tr><tr><td><code>game\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game identifier</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "calls": \[\
        {\
            "rtp": 75615,\
            "call\_type": "Free"\
        },\
        {\
            "rtp": 62185,\
            "call\_type": "Free"\
        },\
        {\
            "rtp": 45010,\
            "call\_type": "Free"\
        }\
    \]
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="153">Field</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>rtp</code></td><td>RTP value — use this in <strong>Call Apply</strong> as <code>call\_rtp</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>call\_type</code></td><td>Call type label</td></tr></tbody></table>

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "External Error"
}
\`\`\`

# Call Cancel

Cancels a waiting call. Use call\\\_id from the Get Call History response.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`call\_cancel\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "call\_cancel",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "call\_id": 109
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="126">Field</th><th width="109">Type</th><th width="121">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>call\_cancel</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>call\_id</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>Call ID from <strong>Get Call History</strong></td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "canceled\_money": 720160,
    "msg": "SUCCESS"
}
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "There is no waiting call."
}
\`\`\`

# In-Game History

Returns a provider-hosted URL where you can view a player's game history for a specific game.

Unlike \`get\_game\_log\`, this does not return transaction records directly — it returns a link to the provider's history page.

> \*\*Availability:\*\* Currently supported for \*\*Pragmatic Play Slot\*\* only (\`provider\_code\`: \`PRAGMATIC\`). Other providers will return \`INVALID\_PROVIDER\`.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`get\_game\_history\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "get\_game\_history",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
    "game\_code": "vs20doghouse"
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="145">Field</th><th width="89">Type</th><th width="117">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>get\_game\_history</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Player identifier</td></tr><tr><td><code>provider\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>PRAGMATIC</code> (Pragmatic Play Slot only)</td></tr><tr><td><code>game\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Pragmatic Play slot game code (e.g. <code>vs20doghouse</code>)</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "history\_url": "https://game-server.example.com/gs2c/lastGameHistory.do?symbol=vs20doghouse&mgckey=..."
}
\`\`\`

| Field         | Description                                                 |
| ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`history\_url\` | URL to open the provider's game history page for the player |

#### Failure

\*\*Invalid provider (not Pragmatic Play Slot):\*\*

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "INVALID\_PROVIDER"
}
\`\`\`

\*\*External provider error:\*\*

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "EXTERNAL\_ERROR : INVALID\_GAME"
}
\`\`\`

\*\*Internal error:\*\*

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "INTERNAL\_ERROR"
}
\`\`\`

# Transaction (Site Endpoint)

Called when a bet or win occurs. Your backend must update the user balance and return the updated value.

## Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{YOUR\_SITE}/gold\_api
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

\*\*Method:\*\* \`transaction\`

## Request

### Slot transaction

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "transaction",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_secret": "your\_agent\_secret",
    "agent\_balance": 10000000,
    "user\_code": "test",
    "user\_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
    "user\_balance": 99200,
    "game\_type": "slot",
    "slot": {
        "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
        "game\_code": "vs20midas",
        "type": "BASE",
        "bet\_money": 1000,
        "win\_money": 200,
        "round\_id": 63792613432127,
        "txn\_id": "63792613432127",
        "txn\_id\_v2": "64a83f2fc597acc9004eec52c3f84c30",
        "txn\_type": "debit\_credit"
    }
}
\`\`\`

### Live transaction

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "transaction",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_secret": "your\_agent\_secret",
    "agent\_balance": 10000000,
    "user\_code": "test",
    "user\_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
    "user\_balance": 102000,
    "game\_type": "live",
    "info": "{\\"gameresult\\":{\\"pp\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"bb\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"gameType\\":\\"baccarat\\",\\"perfectPair\\":\\"false\\",\\"banker\_pair\\":\\"false\\",\\"code\\":\\"0\\",\\"bighand\\":\\"false\\",\\"bg\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"player\_pair\\":\\"false\\",\\"bp\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"result\\":\\"player\\",\\"score\\":\\"8\\",\\"bnc\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"sm\\":\\"2.5\\",\\"id\\":\\"15676627318\\",\\"table\\":\\"pwnhicogrzeodk79\\",\\"eitherPair\\":\\"false\\",\\"gameId\\":\\"15676627318\\",\\"b\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"s6\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"natural\\":\\"true\\",\\"playerBonus\\":\\"true\\",\\"pnc\\":\\"2.0\\",\\"ep\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"bankerBonus\\":\\"false\\",\\"prp\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"p\\":\\"2.0\\",\\"pb\\":\\"2.0\\",\\"t\\":\\"0.0\\",\\"super6\\":\\"false\\",\\"time\\":\\"00:40:15\\",\\"smallhand\\":\\"true\\",\\"seq\\":2304,\\"value\\":\\"player\\"}}",
    "live": {
        "provider\_code": "EVOLUTION",
        "game\_code": "100",
        "type": "baccarat",
        "bet\_money": 2000,
        "win\_money": 4000,
        "round\_id": 15676627318,
        "txn\_id": "15676627318",
        "txn\_id\_v2": "b7e12d4a8f0c4519a2d6e3b81f5c9047",
        "txn\_type": "debit\_credit"
    }
}
\`\`\`

### Sportsbook transaction

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "transaction",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_secret": "your\_agent\_secret",
    "agent\_balance": 10000000,
    "user\_code": "test",
    "user\_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
    "user\_balance": 102000,
    "game\_type": "SB",
    "info": "{\\"couponCode\\":\\"000000561435\\",\\"status\\":\\"pending\\",\\"totalOdds\\":3.73,\\"potentialWin\\":18.64,\\"stake\\":5,\\"payout\\":0,\\"betslips\\":\[{\\"id\\":120,\\"sportName\\":\\"Football\\",\\"countryName\\":\\"World\\",\\"leagueName\\":\\"Club Friendlies\\",\\"matchName\\":\\"Odds - Stromsgodset\\",\\"homeTeam\\":\\"Odds\\",\\"awayTeam\\":\\"Stromsgodset\\",\\"marketName\\":\\"1X2\\",\\"oddName\\":\\"W1\\",\\"oddRate\\":1.46,\\"oddPoint\\":\\"696729566|1|1|0\\"}\],\\"settlementData\\":{}}",
    "SB": {
        "provider\_code": "SPORTSBOOK",
        "game\_code": "SPORTSBOOK",
        "type": "accumulator",
        "bet\_money": 5,
        "win\_money": 0,
        "round\_id": 561435,
        "txn\_id": "561435",
        "txn\_id\_v2": "44097c196a8b59b7371ee2ca6fe83999",
        "txn\_type": "debit"
    }
}
\`\`\`

### Mini game (MN) transaction

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "transaction",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_secret": "your\_agent\_secret",
    "agent\_balance": 10000000,
    "user\_code": "test",
    "user\_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
    "user\_balance": 99200,
    "game\_type": "MN",
    "MN": {
        "provider\_code": "SPRIBE",
        "game\_code": "minigame\_01",
        "type": "BASE",
        "bet\_money": 1000,
        "win\_money": 200,
        "round\_id": 123456789,
        "txn\_id": "123456789",
        "txn\_id\_v2": "3f0a6c1de95b47829c04a7be2d18f635",
        "txn\_type": "debit\_credit"
    }
}
\`\`\`

### Game object fields

\*\*Important:\*\* The game detail object key \*\*must match\*\* the \`game\_type\` value.

> Example: if \`"game\_type": "slot"\`, the payload must include a \`"slot": { ... }\` object. If \`"game\_type": "SB"\`, use \`"SB": { ... }\`.

| game\\\_type | Provider Type |
| ---------- | ------------- |
| \`slot\`     | Slot          |
| \`live\`     | Live Casino   |
| \`SB\`       | Sportsbook    |
| \`MN\`       | Mini Game     |
| \`FT\`       | Fish          |

Fields inside the game object (\`slot\` / \`live\` / \`SB\` / \`MN\`):

<table><thead><tr><th width="144">Field</th><th width="90">Type</th><th width="90">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>provider\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game provider code</td></tr><tr><td><code>game\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Game identifier</td></tr><tr><td><code>type</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Bet/round type (e.g. <code>BASE</code>, <code>baccarat</code>)</td></tr><tr><td><code>bet\_money</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>Bet amount</td></tr><tr><td><code>win\_money</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>Win amount</td></tr><tr><td><code>round\_id</code></td><td>integer</td><td>Yes</td><td>Unique ID for one bet. Every transaction the bet produces shares it</td></tr><tr><td><code>txn\_id</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td><p>The <code>round\_id</code> as a string. </p><p>Starting October 15, 2026 (UTC):<br><code>txn\_id</code> will use the same value as <code>txn\_id\_v2</code>.</p></td></tr><tr><td><code>txn\_id\_v2</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Unique per inner spin of one bet. Deduplicate on this field. <code>txn\_id\_v2</code> will be deprecated on October 31, 2026 (UTC).</td></tr><tr><td><code>txn\_type</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td><code>debit</code>, <code>credit</code>, or <code>debit\_credit</code></td></tr></tbody></table>

> \*\*\`round\_id\` and \`txn\_id\_v2\`\*\*
>
> One bet is not always one payout. A bet can play out over several \*\*inner spins\*\* — tumbling wins, free game rounds, the closing collect — and every one of them is sent as its own transaction.
>
> \* \`round\_id\` — one bet (integer). Every transaction that bet produces carries the same value.
> \* \`txn\_id\_v2\` — unique per inner spin (string). No two transactions ever share it.
>
> \*\*Deduplicate on \`txn\_id\_v2\`.\*\* \`round\_id\` is unique per \*bet\*, not per transaction, so treating it as an idempotency key makes your site drop the win of a round it already booked the bet for.

### Live / Sportsbook \`info\` field

The \`info\` field (top-level, for live and sportsbook) is a \*\*stringified JSON\*\* object with bet/result details.

\*\*Sportsbook \`info\` fields:\*\*

| Field            | Description                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`couponCode\`     | Bet coupon code                                                                                                              |
| \`status\`         | \`pending\`, \`won\`, \`lost\`, \`cashedout\`, or \`canceled\`                                                                         |
| \`totalOdds\`      | Combined odds                                                                                                                |
| \`potentialWin\`   | Potential payout                                                                                                             |
| \`stake\`          | Bet stake                                                                                                                    |
| \`payout\`         | Actual payout                                                                                                                |
| \`betslips\`       | Array of \`{ id, sportName, countryName, leagueName, matchName, homeTeam, awayTeam, marketName, oddName, oddRate, oddPoint }\` |
| \`settlementData\` | Settlement details object                                                                                                    |

## Transaction Type Reference

\`txn\_type\` values: \`debit\`, \`credit\`, \`debit\_credit\`

| Type           | Description             | \`bet\_money\`         | \`win\_money\`         |
| -------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------- | ------------------- |
| \`debit\`        | Only send bet amount    | Valid               | Invalid (don't use) |
| \`credit\`       | Only send win amount    | Invalid (don't use) | Valid               |
| \`debit\_credit\` | Send bet and win amount | Valid               | Valid               |

### Txn examples

One bet (\`round\_id\`, and the \`txn\_id\` mirroring it) can produce several transactions, each with its own \`txn\_id\_v2\`.

\`\`\`json
// debit — bet only
{ "txn\_type": "debit", "bet\_money": 2000, "win\_money": 0, "round\_id": 123456789, "txn\_id": "123456789", "txn\_id\_v2": "64a83f2fc597acc9004eec52c3f84c30" }

// credit — win of an inner spin (same round\_id and txn\_id, new txn\_id\_v2)
{ "txn\_type": "credit", "bet\_money": 0, "win\_money": 1500, "round\_id": 123456789, "txn\_id": "123456789", "txn\_id\_v2": "5c81b0af73e2496db1fa08c6d437e9a2" }

// credit — win of the next inner spin of that same bet
{ "txn\_type": "credit", "bet\_money": 0, "win\_money": 400, "round\_id": 123456789, "txn\_id": "123456789", "txn\_id\_v2": "e04fd920a1b74c8fb2c3576ad8e1490f" }

// debit\_credit — the next bet, settled in one transaction
{ "txn\_type": "debit\_credit", "bet\_money": 2000, "win\_money": 1500, "round\_id": 123456790, "txn\_id": "123456790", "txn\_id\_v2": "a1b2c3d4e5f6789012345678abcdef01" }
\`\`\`

## Response

### Success

Must return the updated user balance:

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "user\_balance": 1000
}
\`\`\`

### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "INSUFFICIENT\_USER\_FUNDS"
}
\`\`\`

# Create Tour

Grants a user a bundle of free rounds (a "free tour") on one slot game. The whole tour is laid out in advance when it is created, so the response already tells you what it will pay.

Only one tour can run per user and game at a time — the previous one has to finish, expire or be canceled first.

> \*\*\`PRAGMATIC\` only for now.\*\* Free tours are supported on Pragmatic games and nowhere else yet. Sending \`tour\_create\` for any other \`provider\_code\` fails, since that provider's game server has no tour to create. More providers are planned.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`tour\_create\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "tour\_create",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
    "game\_code": "vs20doghouse",
    "bet\_level": 1,
    "spin\_count": 10,
    "amount": 70000,
    "expiration\_time": "2026-02-26T06:46:04.000Z",
    "tour\_id": "free\_tour\_777"
}
\`\`\`

| Field             | Type     | Required | Description                                                                                         |
| ----------------- | -------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`method\`          | string   | Yes      | Must be \`tour\_create\`                                                                               |
| \`agent\_code\`      | string   | Yes      | Your agent code                                                                                     |
| \`agent\_token\`     | string   | Yes      | Your agent API token                                                                                |
| \`user\_code\`       | string   | Yes      | Target user                                                                                         |
| \`provider\_code\`   | string   | Yes      | Game provider code                                                                                  |
| \`game\_code\`       | string   | Yes      | Game identifier                                                                                     |
| \`bet\_level\`       | integer  | Yes      | Index into the game's bet range: \`1\` = minimum bet, \`2\` = the next one, and so on                   |
| \`spin\_count\`      | integer  | Yes      | Number of free rounds. \`1\` – \`1000\`                                                                 |
| \`amount\`          | number   | Yes      | Max win of the whole tour, in your currency. At least the equivalent of 7 USD at your currency rate |
| \`expiration\_time\` | datetime | Yes      | When the tour stops being playable. Must be in the future                                           |
| \`tour\_id\`         | string   | Yes      | Your own identifier for the tour, 1 – 36 characters. It comes back in the later callbacks           |

> \*\*\`amount\` is a ceiling, not a target.\*\* The tour is shaped like an ordinary session — only a few of its rounds pay — so what it really pays out is usually below \`amount\`. That figure comes back as \`real\_win\`.

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "tour\_id": "free\_tour\_777",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",
    "game\_code": "vs20doghouse",
    "bet\_level": 1,
    "bet": 100,
    "total\_bet": 2000,
    "spin\_count": 10,
    "amount": 70000,
    "real\_win": 64350,
    "expiration\_time": "2026-02-26T06:46:04.000Z",
    "msg": "SUCCESS"
}
\`\`\`

| Field       | Type   | Description                                           |
| ----------- | ------ | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| \`bet\`       | number | Coin bet of one line at \`bet\_level\`                   |
| \`total\_bet\` | number | Bet of one free round (\`bet\` × the game's pay lines)  |
| \`real\_win\`  | number | What the prepared rounds actually pay, up to \`amount\` |

\*\*Balance:\*\* \`real\_win\` is deducted from your agent balance the moment the tour is created, and the unplayed part comes back if you cancel it. Your balance still has to cover the full \`amount\` at creation time, before \`real\_win\` is known.

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "Duplicated Tour Id."
}
\`\`\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "External Error",
    "detail": "INVALID\_BET\_LEVEL"
}
\`\`\`

\`External Error\` means the game server refused the tour. \`detail\` says why:

| \`detail\`                  | Description                                              |
| ------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| \`INVALID\_GAME\`            | The game does not exist or has no pay lines              |
| \`PO\_GAME\`                 | The game is switched off                                 |
| \`INVALID\_BET\_LEVEL\`       | \`bet\_level\` is above the game's highest bet              |
| \`INVALID\_SPIN\_COUNT\`      | \`spin\_count\` is outside \`1\` – \`1000\`                     |
| \`INVALID\_AMOUNT\`          | \`amount\` is below the 7 USD equivalent for your currency |
| \`INVALID\_EXPIRATION\_TIME\` | \`expiration\_time\` is missing or already past             |
| \`INVALID\_TOUR\_ID\`         | \`tour\_id\` is longer than 36 characters                   |
| \`DUPLICATED\_TOUR\_ID\`      | The game server already knows this \`tour\_id\`             |
| \`TOUR\_IN\_PROGRESS\`        | This user still has a playable tour on this game         |
| \`NO\_PATTERN\_DATA\`         | The game has no round data to build a tour from          |

A refused tour is not recorded, so the same \`tour\_id\` can be used again once the cause is fixed.


---

# Unknown

\> For the complete documentation index, see \[llms.txt\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/llms.txt). Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/call-api/call-history.md).

# Call History

Returns paginated call history for your agent.

### Endpoint

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

\*\*Content-Type:\*\* \`application/json\`

### Request

\*\*Method:\*\* \`call\_history\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "call\_history",
    "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",
    "agent\_token": "your\_agent\_token",
    "offset": 0,
    "limit": 100
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="124">Field</th><th width="108">Type</th><th width="111">Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Must be <code>call\_history</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent code</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Yes</td><td>Your agent API token</td></tr><tr><td><code>offset</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>Pagination offset (starting index)</td></tr><tr><td><code>limit</code></td><td>number</td><td>Yes</td><td>Number of records to return</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Response

#### Success

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1,
    "data": \[\
        {\
            "id": 93,\
            "agent\_code": "your\_agent\_code",\
            "user\_code": "test",\
            "provider\_code": "PRAGMATIC",\
            "game\_code": "vs20doghouse",\
            "bet": 1600,\
            "user\_prev": null,\
            "user\_after": null,\
            "agent\_prev": 901200,\
            "agent\_after": 181040,\
            "expect": 720160,\
            "missed": 720160,\
            "real": 0,\
            "rtp": 45010,\
            "type": "common",\
            "status": 0,\
            "msg": null,\
            "created\_at": "2023-02-23T04:02:45.000Z",\
            "updated\_at": "2023-02-23T04:02:46.000Z"\
        }\
    \]
}
\`\`\`

#### Failure

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "Invalid Agent."
}
\`\`\`

### Call Status Reference

<table><thead><tr><th width="144">Status</th><th>Meaning</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>0</code></td><td>Waiting</td></tr><tr><td><code>1</code></td><td>Processing</td></tr><tr><td><code>2</code></td><td>Finished</td></tr><tr><td><code>3</code></td><td>Rejected by game server (bet amount changed)</td></tr><tr><td><code>4</code></td><td>Canceled (via Cancel API or back-office button)</td></tr></tbody></table>

### Expect, Missed, and Real Money

<table><thead><tr><th width="150">Field</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>expect</code></td><td>Amount reserved when the admin applies the call (e.g. 1000)</td></tr><tr><td><code>real</code></td><td>Amount the user actually received (e.g. 800)</td></tr><tr><td><code>missed</code></td><td>Difference returned to the admin (<code>expect − real</code>, e.g. 200)</td></tr></tbody></table>

#### Why missed money?

Some Pragmatic Play games with free-option mechanics (e.g. 5 Lions Megaways, The Dog House Megaways, Wild Booster) may not deliver the full expected payout. Unused funds are returned to the agent.

### Status Examples

#### Waiting (\`status: 0\`)

\`\`\`json
{
    "id": 93,
    "status": 0,
    "expect": 720160,
    "missed": 720160,
    "real": 0,
    "msg": null
}
\`\`\`

#### Processing (\`status: 1\`)

\`\`\`json
{
    "id": 94,
    "status": 1,
    "msg": "Processing"
}
\`\`\`

#### Finished (\`status: 2\`)

\`\`\`json
{
    "id": 95,
    "status": 2,
    "expect": 2000,
    "missed": 0,
    "real": 2000,
    "msg": "Finished"
}
\`\`\`

#### Rejected — bet changed (\`status: 3\`)

\`\`\`json
{
    "id": 106,
    "status": 3,
    "msg": "Rejected (callBet = 2000, currentBet = 1600)}"
}
\`\`\`

#### Canceled (\`status: 4\`)

\`\`\`json
{
    "id": 107,
    "status": 4,
    "msg": "Canceled by User"
}
\`\`\`

Markdown versions of documentation pages are available by appending \`.md\` to page URLs; this page is available as \[Markdown\](https://ggr.gitbook.io/docs/integration/transfer-api/deposit-withdraw-user-balance.md).

# Deposit / Withdraw User Balance

## End\*\*point\*\*

\`\`\`
POST https://{API\_SERVER}
\`\`\`

## \*\*Request Body\*\*

<pre class="language-json"><code class="lang-json"><strong>{
</strong>    "method": "user\_deposit",
    "agent\_code": "testAgent",
    "agent\_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "amount": 10000.00,
    "agent\_sign"："UNIQUE\_ID"   // Optional to prevent duplicated
}
</code></pre>

\`\`\`json
{
    "method": "user\_withdraw",
    "agent\_code": "testAgent",
    "agent\_token": "c3041c6d9146a164387fa454eff7b432",
    "user\_code": "test",
    "amount": 10000.00,
    "agent\_sign"："UNIQUE\_ID"   // Optional to prevent duplicated
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="130">Key</th><th width="96">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>method</code></td><td>string</td><td>API method name. For balance transfer to a user game wallet, the value must be <code>user\_deposit</code> or  <code>user\_withdraw</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Unique agent identifier provided by NEXUS. Used to identify which agent is sending the request.</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_token</code></td><td>string</td><td>Secret authentication token assigned to the agent. Used for API authentication and request validation.</td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_code</code></td><td>string</td><td>Unique user identifier within the agent’s platform. This user will receive the transferred balance.</td></tr><tr><td><code>amount</code></td><td>double</td><td>Amount to transfer from the agent balance to the user API balance. Supports up to 2 digits after the decimal point. Example: <code>10000.00</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_sign</code></td><td>string</td><td>Optional unique request identifier used to prevent duplicated requests. Recommended for transaction safety and idempotency handling. Example: UUID, hash string, timestamp-based unique value.</td></tr></tbody></table>

## \*\*Response Example\*\*

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 1, 
    "msg": "SUCCESS",
    "agent\_balance": 990000,
    "user\_balance": 10000
}
\`\`\`

\`\`\`json
{
    "status": 0,
    "msg": "INSUFFICIENT\_AGENT\_FUNDS" / "INSUFFICIENT\_USER\_FUNDS"
}
\`\`\`

<table><thead><tr><th width="151">Field</th><th width="86">Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>status</code></td><td>int</td><td>Request processing status. <code>1</code> indicates success, while <code>0</code> indicates failure.</td></tr><tr><td><code>msg</code></td><td>string</td><td>Response message describing the result of the request. Example:  <code>SUCCESS: "INSUFFICIENT\_AGENT\_FUNDS"</code>.</td></tr><tr><td><code>agent\_balance</code></td><td>double</td><td>Remaining balance of the agent after the transaction has been processed successfully. Supports up to 2 digits after the decimal point. </td></tr><tr><td><code>user\_balance</code></td><td>double</td><td>Updated API balance of the user after the transaction has been completed successfully. Supports up to 2 digits after the decimal point. </td></tr></tbody></table>
